<script id="ethvelle-v71-editor-fixes-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const safeNum=(v,fallback=0)=>{const n=Number(v);return Number.isFinite(n)?n:fallback;};
  const clamp=(v,min,max)=>Math.max(min,Math.min(max,safeNum(v,min)));
  const esc=v=>String(v==null?'':v).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));

  /* ---------- Clip Tracking: attach a selected image/video layer to another timeline clip ---------- */
  function charKey(ch,index){return ch&&ch.id!=null?String(ch.id):'index:'+index;}
  /* v339: the clip being edited can be ANY visual clip: image / video / text / effect clips AND the
     animation clips of a character ('main' / 'segN'). Both kinds store their config in `clipTracking`. */
  function getTrackingOverlay(){
    const sel=selectedClip;
    if(sel==null)return null;
    const ov=Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays.find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===String(sel)&&['image','video','text'].includes(o.type)):null;
    if(ov)return ov;
    if(sel==='main'||/^seg\d+$/.test(String(sel))){
      const clip=typeof getClipByRef==='function'?getClipByRef(String(sel)):null;
      return clip&&typeof clip==='object'?clip:null;
    }
    return null;
  }
  /* ---------- v339: animation clips as tracking followers / sources ---------- */
  function allAnimClips(){
    const out=[],chars=Array.isArray(characters)?characters:[];
    chars.forEach((ch,idx)=>{
      if(!ch)return;
      const list=[];
      if(ch.clip&&typeof ch.clip==='object')list.push({ref:'main',clip:ch.clip});
      (Array.isArray(ch.segments)?ch.segments:[]).forEach((seg,i)=>{if(seg&&typeof seg==='object')list.push({ref:'seg'+i,clip:seg});});
      const used=new Set();
      list.forEach(e=>{
        // Animation clips have no id of their own. Give each a stable one (saved with the clip);
        // a split/duplicate copies the id, so the later copy is re-issued a fresh one.
        if(typeof e.clip.evTrackId!=='string'||!e.clip.evTrackId||used.has(e.clip.evTrackId)){
          e.clip.evTrackId='at'+Date.now().toString(36)+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7);
        }
        used.add(e.clip.evTrackId);
        out.push({ch,idx,ref:e.ref,clip:e.clip,key:'anim:'+charKey(ch,idx)+'|'+e.clip.evTrackId});
      });
    });
    return out;
  }
  function trackingFollowerKey(f){
    if(!f)return '';
    const hit=allAnimClips().find(e=>e.clip===f);
    return hit?hit.key:'ov:'+String(f.id);
  }
  function trackingAnimCharPart(key){const k=String(key||'');return k.slice(5,k.lastIndexOf('|'));}
  function trackingTargetMatches(targetId,ref){
    const t=String(targetId||''),r=String(ref||'');
    if(t===r)return true;
    if(r.indexOf('char:')===0&&t.indexOf('anim:')===0)return trackingAnimCharPart(t)===r.slice(5);
    return false;
  }
  function trackingSameNode(a,b){
    a=String(a);b=String(b);
    if(a===b)return true;
    return a.indexOf('anim:')===0&&b.indexOf('anim:')===0&&trackingAnimCharPart(a)===trackingAnimCharPart(b);
  }
  function trackingNodeNext(key){
    const out=[],k=String(key||'');
    if(k.indexOf('ov:')===0){
      const id=k.slice(3),clip=(Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===id);
      if(!clip)return out;
      const cfg=clip.clipTracking;
      if(cfg&&cfg.enabled&&cfg.targetId)out.push(String(cfg.targetId));
      if(clip.ethvelleEffectClip&&clip.ethvelleEffectKind==='character'){
        allAnimClips().forEach(e=>{if(String(e.ch.id)===String(clip.ethvelleEffectCharId))out.push(e.key);});
      }
    }else if(k.indexOf('anim:')===0){
      const all=allAnimClips(),hit=all.find(e=>e.key===k);
      if(hit)all.forEach(e=>{if(e.ch===hit.ch){const c=e.clip.clipTracking;if(c&&c.enabled&&c.targetId)out.push(String(c.targetId));}});
    }
    return out;
  }
  // True when `startKey` (a candidate source) already follows — directly or through a chain —
  // the clip being edited. Offering it would create a loop (A follows B follows A).
  function trackingDependsOn(startKey,goalKey){
    const seen=new Set(),stack=[String(startKey)];
    while(stack.length){
      const k=stack.pop();
      if(seen.has(k))continue;seen.add(k);
      if(trackingSameNode(k,goalKey))return true;
      trackingNodeNext(k).forEach(n=>stack.push(n));
    }
    return false;
  }
  function trackingThumbForAnim(ch,clip){
    const preset=(typeof animPresets!=='undefined'&&animPresets)?animPresets[clip.animKey]:null;
    const name=String((preset&&preset.name)||'Animation').slice(0,22);
    const who=String((ch&&ch.name)||'Character').slice(0,22);
    const e=v=>v.replace(/[&<>"]/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]));
    const svg='<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 320 180"><rect width="320" height="180" fill="#243B80"/><g stroke="#fff" stroke-width="5" stroke-linecap="round" fill="none"><circle cx="160" cy="42" r="14"/><path d="M160 56v38M160 68l-24 18M160 68l24 18M160 94l-19 32M160 94l19 32"/></g><text x="160" y="152" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Arial" font-size="19" font-weight="700" fill="#fff">'+e(name)+'</text><text x="160" y="171" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Arial" font-size="12" fill="#C9D3FF">'+e(who)+'</text></svg>';
    return 'data:image/svg+xml;charset=UTF-8,'+encodeURIComponent(svg);
  }
  function trackingNow(){
    try{return typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?safeNum(getCurrentAbsoluteTime(),0):safeNum(currentAbsoluteTimeSec,0);}catch(_e){return safeNum(currentAbsoluteTimeSec,0);}
  }
  function trackingSnapshot(raw){
    const t=raw||{};
    return {scale:Math.max(.0001,safeNum(t.scale,1)),offsetX:safeNum(t.offsetX,0),offsetY:safeNum(t.offsetY,0),rotation:safeNum(t.rotation,0)};
  }
  function trackingAngleDelta(from,to){return ((safeNum(to)-safeNum(from)+540)%360)-180;}
  function combineTrackingDelta(base,source,rest,cfg){
    const b=trackingSnapshot(base),s=trackingSnapshot(source),r=trackingSnapshot(rest);
    const followScale=!cfg||cfg.followScale!==false;
    const followRotation=!cfg||cfg.followRotation!==false;
    return {
      scale:b.scale*(followScale?s.scale/Math.max(.0001,r.scale):1),
      offsetX:b.offsetX+(s.offsetX-r.offsetX)+safeNum(cfg&&cfg.offsetX,0),
      offsetY:b.offsetY+(s.offsetY-r.offsetY)+safeNum(cfg&&cfg.offsetY,0),
      rotation:b.rotation+(followRotation?trackingAngleDelta(r.rotation,s.rotation):0)
    };
  }
  function trackingRangesOverlap(a,b){
    if(!a||!b)return false;
    const as=Number(a.trimStart),ae=Number(a.trimEnd),bs=Number(b.trimStart),be=Number(b.trimEnd);
    return [as,ae,bs,be].every(Number.isFinite)&&Math.max(as,bs)<Math.min(ae,be)-0.0001;
  }
  function unwrapTrackingThumb(src){
    let value=String(src||'').trim();
    if(/^url\(/i.test(value))value=value.replace(/^url\(\s*["']?/i,'').replace(/["']?\s*\)$/,'');
    return value;
  }
  function trackingThumbForOverlay(clip){
    if(!clip)return '';
    if(clip.type==='image')return String((clip.thumbs&&clip.thumbs[0])||clip.poster||clip.src||'');
    if(clip.type==='video'){
      // Use the cached frame at the source timestamp shown FIRST by this timeline clip.
      // The video may be reversed or trimmed, so the first timeline frame is not always t=0.
      const sourceFrames=window.__evSourceFrames;
      if(sourceFrames&&typeof sourceFrames.setFor==='function'&&typeof sourceFrames.nearest==='function'){
        try{
          const set=sourceFrames.setFor(clip);
          if(set&&set.times&&set.times.length){
            const span=Math.max(0,(Number(clip.trimEnd)-Number(clip.trimStart))*(Number(clip.speed)||1));
            let firstSourceTime=Math.max(0,Number(clip.sourceOffset)||0);
            if(clip.reversePlayback)firstSourceTime+=span;
            const duration=Math.max(0,Number(clip.sourceDuration)||Number(set.duration)||0);
            if(duration>0)firstSourceTime=Math.min(firstSourceTime,Math.max(0,duration-.04));
            const cached=sourceFrames.nearest(set,firstSourceTime);
            if(cached)return String(cached);
          }
        }catch(_e){}
      }
      const id=String(clip.id);
      const node=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#timelineInner .overlay-clip')).find(el=>String(el.dataset.overlayId||'')===id);
      // The strip can be virtualized while scrolled. Accept only tile zero, never an arbitrary
      // visible middle frame, so the tracking card always represents the clip's first frame.
      const tile=node&&node.querySelector('.ev-video-thumb-tile[data-ev-thumb-index="0"].ev-video-thumb-ready');
      if(tile)return String(tile.dataset.evThumbUrl||unwrapTrackingThumb(getComputedStyle(tile).backgroundImage)||'');
      // A supplied poster is only a fallback; prefer a real cached first frame whenever present.
      return String(clip.poster||clip.thumbnail||'');
    }
    if(clip.type==='text'){
      const label=String(clip.textContent||clip.name||'Text').slice(0,36);
      const svg='<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 320 180"><rect width="320" height="180" fill="#242735"/><text x="160" y="98" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Arial" font-size="24" font-weight="700" fill="#fff">'+label.replace(/[&<>"]/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]))+'</text></svg>';
      return 'data:image/svg+xml;charset=UTF-8,'+encodeURIComponent(svg);
    }
    return '';
  }
  function trackingThumbForBackground(clip){
    if(!clip)return '';
    const src=unwrapTrackingThumb(clip.thumbnail||clip.src||clip.image||'');
    return src;
  }
  function trackingAspectRatio(clip){
    if(!clip)return 16/9;
    let el=null;
    try{
      if(clip.id!=null&&typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls)el=overlayMediaEls[clip.id]||null;
      if(!el&&String(clip.id||'').startsWith('bg'))el=document.getElementById('bgImgLayer');
    }catch(_e){}
    const w=Number(clip.sourceWidth)||Number(el&&(el.videoWidth||el.naturalWidth||el.width))||Number(clip.naturalWidth)||Number(clip.width)||0;
    const h=Number(clip.sourceHeight)||Number(el&&(el.videoHeight||el.naturalHeight||el.height))||Number(clip.naturalHeight)||Number(clip.height)||0;
    const explicit=Number(clip.aspectRatio||clip.ratio)||0;
    const ratio=(w>0&&h>0)?w/h:explicit>0?explicit:16/9;
    return Number.isFinite(ratio)&&ratio>0?Math.max(.18,Math.min(5.5,ratio)):16/9;
  }
  function trackingThumbBox(ratio){
    const r=Math.max(.18,Math.min(5.5,Number(ratio)||16/9));
    const h=Math.min(54,118/r),w=h*r;
    return {width:Math.round(w),height:Math.round(h)};
  }
  function getTrackingCandidates(follower){
    /* v339: a clip can follow a clip from ANY section — Background, Layer/Media, Text, Effects and
       the Animation (character) rows — as long as the two overlap in time. */
    const followerKey=trackingFollowerKey(follower);
    const anims=allAnimClips();
    const followerAnim=anims.find(e=>e.clip===follower)||null;
    const chars=Array.isArray(characters)?characters:[];
    const fxChar=o=>(o&&o.ethvelleEffectClip&&o.ethvelleEffectKind==='character')?(chars.find(c=>c&&String(c.id)===String(o.ethvelleEffectCharId))||null):null;
    const followerFxChar=fxChar(follower);
    const backgroundsList=Array.isArray(backgrounds)?backgrounds:[];
    const overlaysList=Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[];
    const byTimeline=(a,b)=>(Number(a.trimStart)||0)-(Number(b.trimStart)||0)||String(a.id).localeCompare(String(b.id));
    const isFx=o=>!!(o&&o.ethvelleEffectClip);
    const notSelf=o=>o&&o.id!=null&&('ov:'+String(o.id))!==followerKey;
    const bgItems=backgroundsList
      .filter(b=>b&&b.id!=null&&b!==follower&&trackingRangesOverlap(b,follower))
      .sort(byTimeline)
      .map((b,i)=>({value:'bg:'+String(b.id),kind:'background',thumb:trackingThumbForBackground(b),ratio:trackingAspectRatio(b),label:'Background row clip '+(i+1),clipId:String(b.id)}));
    const overlayClips=overlaysList
      .filter(o=>notSelf(o)&&['image','video'].includes(o.type)&&!isFx(o)&&trackingRangesOverlap(o,follower))
      .sort(byTimeline);
    const textClips=overlaysList
      .filter(o=>notSelf(o)&&o.type==='text'&&trackingRangesOverlap(o,follower))
      .sort(byTimeline);
    const fxClips=overlaysList
      .filter(o=>notSelf(o)&&isFx(o)&&trackingRangesOverlap(o,follower)&&!(followerAnim&&fxChar(o)===followerAnim.ch))
      .sort(byTimeline);
    const animClips=anims
      .filter(e=>e.clip!==follower&&!(followerAnim&&e.ch===followerAnim.ch)&&!(followerFxChar&&e.ch===followerFxChar)&&trackingRangesOverlap(e.clip,follower))
      .sort((a,b)=>(Number(a.clip.trimStart)||0)-(Number(b.clip.trimStart)||0)||a.key.localeCompare(b.key));
    const overlayItems=overlayClips.map((o,i)=>({value:'ov:'+String(o.id),kind:o.type,thumb:trackingThumbForOverlay(o),ratio:trackingAspectRatio(o),label:'Layer row clip '+(i+1),clipId:String(o.id)}));
    const textItems=textClips.map((o,i)=>({value:'ov:'+String(o.id),kind:o.type,thumb:trackingThumbForOverlay(o),ratio:trackingAspectRatio(o),label:'Text row clip '+(i+1),clipId:String(o.id)}));
    const fxItems=fxClips.map((o,i)=>({value:'ov:'+String(o.id),kind:'effect',thumb:trackingThumbForOverlay(o),ratio:trackingAspectRatio(o),label:(o.ethvelleEffectKind==='character'?'Character effect ':'Effect ')+(i+1),clipId:String(o.id)}));
    const animItems=animClips.map((e,i)=>({value:e.key,kind:'animation',thumb:trackingThumbForAnim(e.ch,e.clip),ratio:16/9,label:'Animation row clip '+(i+1),clipId:String(e.clip.evTrackId)}));
    return [
      {label:'Background',items:bgItems},
      {label:'Layer',items:overlayItems},
      {label:'Text',items:textItems},
      {label:'Animation',items:animItems},
      {label:'Effects',items:fxItems}
    ].map(group=>({...group,items:group.items.filter(item=>!!item.thumb&&!trackingDependsOn(item.value,followerKey))})).filter(group=>group.items.length);
  }
  function getTrackingSourceTransform(ref,time,seen){
    const key=String(ref||''),t=Number.isFinite(Number(time))?Number(time):trackingNow();
    const visited=seen||new Set();
    if(key.indexOf('bg:')===0){
      const id=key.slice(3),clip=(Array.isArray(backgrounds)?backgrounds:[]).find(b=>b&&String(b.id)===id);
      if(!clip)return null;
      const keyed=typeof window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue==='function'?window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(clip,'transform',t):null;
      return trackingSnapshot(keyed||clip.transform||{});
    }
    if(key.indexOf('char:')===0){
      const id=key.slice(5),idx=(Array.isArray(characters)?characters:[]).findIndex((ch,i)=>charKey(ch,i)===id);
      const ch=idx>=0?characters[idx]:null;
      if(!ch)return null;
      // modelTransform is the live evaluated transform: renderFrame/animateAllCharacters
      // update it from the character's own clip keyframes before overlay media is painted.
      return trackingSnapshot(ch.modelTransform||ch.restTransform||{});
    }
    if(key.indexOf('anim:')===0){
      // An animation clip drives its character's live transform (already tracked, if that clip
      // itself follows something) — read it exactly like the legacy 'char:' source below.
      const hit=allAnimClips().find(e=>e.key===key);
      if(!hit)return null;
      return trackingSnapshot(hit.ch.modelTransform||hit.ch.restTransform||{});
    }
    if(key.indexOf('ov:')===0){
      const id=key.slice(3),clip=(Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===id&&['image','video','text','shape','color'].includes(o.type));
      if(!clip)return null;
      const marker='ov:'+id;
      const keyed=typeof window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue==='function'?window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(clip,'transform',t):null;
      let tr=trackingSnapshot(keyed||clip.transform||{});
      if(visited.has(marker))return tr; // break accidental A→B→A tracking loops safely
      visited.add(marker);
      const cfg=clip.clipTracking;
      if(cfg&&cfg.enabled&&cfg.targetId&&cfg.targetBaseTransform){
        const source=getTrackingSourceTransform(cfg.targetId,t,visited);
        if(source)tr=combineTrackingDelta(tr,source,cfg.targetBaseTransform,cfg);
      }
      visited.delete(marker);
      const motion=typeof window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState==='function'?window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState(clip,t):null;
      if(motion){
        tr.offsetX+=safeNum(motion.x,0);tr.offsetY+=safeNum(motion.y,0);
        tr.rotation+=safeNum(motion.rotation,0);tr.scale*=Math.max(.0001,safeNum(motion.scale,1));
      }
      // v339: a character-effect clip is drawn relative to its character, so its on-screen motion
      // is the character's own transform plus the effect's offset/scale/rotation.
      if(clip.ethvelleEffectClip&&clip.ethvelleEffectKind==='character'){
        const fxCh=(Array.isArray(characters)?characters:[]).find(c=>c&&String(c.id)===String(clip.ethvelleEffectCharId));
        if(fxCh&&fxCh.modelTransform){
          const cm=trackingSnapshot(fxCh.modelTransform);
          tr={scale:tr.scale*cm.scale,offsetX:tr.offsetX+cm.offsetX,offsetY:tr.offsetY+cm.offsetY,rotation:tr.rotation+cm.rotation};
        }
      }
      return tr;
    }
    return null;
  }
  function ensureTrackingConfig(ov){
    if(!ov)return null;
    if(!ov.clipTracking||typeof ov.clipTracking!=='object')ov.clipTracking={enabled:false,targetId:'',targetBaseTransform:null,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,followScale:true,followRotation:true};
    const cfg=ov.clipTracking;
    if(typeof cfg.enabled!=='boolean')cfg.enabled=false;
    if(typeof cfg.targetId!=='string')cfg.targetId='';
    if(!Number.isFinite(Number(cfg.offsetX)))cfg.offsetX=0;
    if(!Number.isFinite(Number(cfg.offsetY)))cfg.offsetY=0;
    if(typeof cfg.followScale!=='boolean')cfg.followScale=true;
    if(typeof cfg.followRotation!=='boolean')cfg.followRotation=true;
    if(cfg.targetBaseTransform&&typeof cfg.targetBaseTransform==='object')cfg.targetBaseTransform=trackingSnapshot(cfg.targetBaseTransform);
    return cfg;
  }
  function captureTrackingBase(cfg,targetId){
    const tr=getTrackingSourceTransform(targetId,trackingNow(),new Set());
    cfg.targetBaseTransform=tr?trackingSnapshot(tr):null;
  }
  window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform=function(ov,time,localBase){
    const t=Number.isFinite(Number(time))?Number(time):trackingNow();
    const keyed=localBase||(typeof window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue==='function'?window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(ov,'transform',t):null);
    const base=trackingSnapshot(keyed||ov&&ov.transform||{});
    const cfg=ov&&ov.clipTracking;
    if(cfg&&cfg.enabled&&cfg.targetId){
      const seen=new Set(['ov:'+String(ov.id)]);
      const source=getTrackingSourceTransform(cfg.targetId,t,seen);
      if(source){
        const rest=cfg.targetBaseTransform||source;
        return combineTrackingDelta(base,source,rest,cfg);
      }
      return base;
    }
    // Backward compatibility: projects made before v235 may still contain characterTracking.
    const legacy=ov&&ov.characterTracking;
    if(!legacy||!legacy.enabled||!Array.isArray(characters)||!characters.length)return base;
    const idx=characters.findIndex((ch,i)=>charKey(ch,i)===String(legacy.characterId));
    if(idx<0)return base;
    const ch=characters[idx],mt=ch.modelTransform||{},rest=ch.restTransform||mt;
    const baseScale=Math.max(.0001,safeNum(rest.scale,1)),currentScale=Math.max(.0001,safeNum(mt.scale,baseScale));
    const dx=safeNum(mt.offsetX)-safeNum(rest.offsetX),dy=safeNum(mt.offsetY)-safeNum(rest.offsetY);
    const hipY=ch.bones&&ch.bones.hip?safeNum(ch.bones.hip.y,safeNum(ch.hipRestY)):safeNum(ch.hipRestY);
    const hipShift=Number.isFinite(Number(ch.hipRestY))?hipY-safeNum(ch.hipRestY):0;
    const dr=safeNum(mt.rotation)-safeNum(rest.rotation);
    return {
      scale:base.scale*(legacy.followScale===false?1:currentScale/baseScale),
      offsetX:base.offsetX+dx+safeNum(legacy.offsetX),
      offsetY:base.offsetY+dy+hipShift+safeNum(legacy.offsetY),
      rotation:base.rotation+(legacy.followRotation?dr:0)
    };
  };
  window.ethvelleRefreshTrackingFollowersForSource=function(sourceRef,time){
    const ref=String(sourceRef||''),t=Number.isFinite(Number(time))?Number(time):trackingNow();
    const list=Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[];
    const layer=$('overlayMediaLayer');
    list.forEach(follower=>{
      const cfg=follower&&follower.clipTracking;
      if(!cfg||!cfg.enabled||!trackingTargetMatches(cfg.targetId,ref))return;
      const id=String(follower.id),live=(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls)?overlayMediaEls[id]:null;
      if(live&&live.isConnected&&typeof window.ethvelleApplyOverlayVisualStyle==='function'){
        window.ethvelleApplyOverlayVisualStyle(live,follower,t,live,false);
        const chroma=(typeof overlayChromaCanvases!=='undefined'&&overlayChromaCanvases)?overlayChromaCanvases[id]:null;
        if(chroma&&chroma.isConnected)window.ethvelleApplyOverlayVisualStyle(chroma,follower,t,live,false);
        const hold=live.__evScrubHoldCanvas;
        if(hold&&hold.isConnected){hold.dataset.evMaskOwnerTime=String(t);window.ethvelleApplyOverlayVisualStyle(hold,follower,t,live,false);}
      }
      if(layer){
        layer.querySelectorAll('[data-ev-mask-owner-id]').forEach(node=>{
          if(String(node.dataset.evMaskOwnerId||'')!==id||node===live)return;
          node.dataset.evMaskOwnerTime=String(t);
          if(typeof window.ethvelleApplyOverlayVisualStyle==='function')window.ethvelleApplyOverlayVisualStyle(node,follower,t,live&&live.isConnected?live:node,false);
        });
      }
    });
  };
  /* v339: animation clips that follow a clip. Runs once per rendered frame from animateAllCharacters(),
     after every character has its authored transform and before anything is painted, so preview,
     scrubbing and export all share it. */
  window.ethvelleApplyCharacterTracking=function(time){
    const chars=Array.isArray(characters)?characters:[];
    const t=Number.isFinite(Number(time))?Number(time):trackingNow();
    const wants=c=>!!(c&&c.clipTracking&&c.clipTracking.enabled&&c.clipTracking.targetId);
    const jobs=[];
    chars.forEach((ch,idx)=>{
      if(!ch||!ch.modelTransform)return;
      ch.__evTrackDelta=null;ch.__evTrackJob=null;
      const segs=Array.isArray(ch.segments)?ch.segments:[];
      if(!(wants(ch.clip)||segs.some(wants)))return;
      let entry=null;
      try{entry=stickmanClipAt(t,ch.clip||null,segs);}catch(_e){}
      const clip=entry&&entry.clip,cfg=clip&&clip.clipTracking;
      if(!wants(clip)||!cfg.targetBaseTransform)return;
      if(!(t>=Number(clip.trimStart)&&t<Number(clip.trimEnd)))return;
      const job={ch,idx,cfg,clip,base:trackingSnapshot(ch.modelTransform)};
      ch.__evTrackJob=job;jobs.push(job);
    });
    if(!jobs.length)return;
    // Several followers can depend on one another (A follows B follows C): re-evaluate from the
    // untouched base a few times so every link settles. A single follower needs only one pass.
    const rounds=jobs.length>1?3:1;
    for(let r=0;r<rounds;r++){
      jobs.forEach(job=>{
        const seen=new Set(['anim:'+charKey(job.ch,job.idx)]);
        const source=getTrackingSourceTransform(job.cfg.targetId,t,seen);
        if(!source)return;
        applyCharJob(job,source);
      });
    }
  };
  function applyCharJob(job,source){
    const next=combineTrackingDelta(job.base,source,job.cfg.targetBaseTransform,job.cfg);
    const mt=job.ch.modelTransform;
    mt.scale=next.scale;mt.offsetX=next.offsetX;mt.offsetY=next.offsetY;mt.rotation=next.rotation;
    job.ch.__evTrackDelta={ds:next.scale/Math.max(.0001,job.base.scale),dx:next.offsetX-job.base.offsetX,dy:next.offsetY-job.base.offsetY,dr:next.rotation-job.base.rotation};
  }
  // Live repaint of animation-clip followers while their source is being dragged.
  const refreshOverlayFollowers=window.ethvelleRefreshTrackingFollowersForSource;
  window.ethvelleRefreshTrackingFollowersForSource=function(sourceRef,time){
    refreshOverlayFollowers.apply(this,arguments);
    try{
      const ref=String(sourceRef||''),t=Number.isFinite(Number(time))?Number(time):trackingNow();
      let touched=false;
      (Array.isArray(characters)?characters:[]).forEach((ch,idx)=>{
        const job=ch&&ch.__evTrackJob;
        if(!job||!job.cfg||!job.cfg.enabled||!trackingTargetMatches(job.cfg.targetId,ref))return;
        const source=getTrackingSourceTransform(job.cfg.targetId,t,new Set(['anim:'+charKey(ch,idx)]));
        if(!source)return;
        applyCharJob(job,source);touched=true;
      });
      if(touched&&typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();
    }catch(_e){}
  };
  // How far tracking moves a clip away from its authored transform (used by selection frames).
  window.ethvelleTrackingDelta=function(ov,time){
    const cfg=ov&&ov.clipTracking;
    if(!cfg||!cfg.enabled||!cfg.targetId||typeof window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform!=='function')return null;
    const t=Number.isFinite(Number(time))?Number(time):trackingNow();
    const keyed=typeof window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue==='function'?window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(ov,'transform',t):null;
    const base=trackingSnapshot(keyed||ov.transform||{});
    const tr=window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform(ov,t,keyed);
    if(!tr)return null;
    return {ds:safeNum(tr.scale,1)/Math.max(.0001,base.scale),dx:safeNum(tr.offsetX)-base.offsetX,dy:safeNum(tr.offsetY)-base.offsetY,dr:safeNum(tr.rotation)-base.rotation};
  };
  function renderTrackingPanel(){
    const host=$('panelBody');if(!host)return;
    const ov=getTrackingOverlay();
    if(!ov){host.innerHTML='';return;}
    const cfg=ensureTrackingConfig(ov),groups=getTrackingCandidates(ov);
    const candidateList=groups.flatMap(g=>g.items);
    if(cfg.targetId&&!candidateList.some(x=>String(x.value)===String(cfg.targetId))){
      cfg.targetId='';cfg.targetBaseTransform=null;cfg.enabled=false;
    }
    if(!cfg.targetId)cfg.enabled=false;
    const rows=groups.map(group=>group.items.map(item=>{
      const selected=String(cfg.targetId)===String(item.value);
      const safeValue=esc(item.value),safeLabel=esc(item.label),safeThumb=esc(item.thumb);
      const box=trackingThumbBox(item.ratio),ratio=Number(item.ratio)||16/9;
      return `<button type="button" class="evv-track-source-card ${selected?'selected':''}" data-track-source="${safeValue}" data-track-source-id="${esc(item.clipId)}" data-track-source-kind="${esc(item.kind)}" aria-label="${safeLabel}" aria-pressed="${selected?'true':'false'}"><span class="evv-track-source-thumb" style="width:${box.width}px;height:${box.height}px;aspect-ratio:${ratio.toFixed(5)}"><img src="${safeThumb}" alt="" loading="lazy" decoding="async"></span><span class="evv-track-source-label">${safeLabel}</span></button>`;
    }).join('')).join('');
    host.innerHTML=`<div class="evv-panel evv-tracking-compact">
      <div class="evv-track-enable-row"><span>Enable tracking</span><button type="button" class="evv-track-enable-switch ${cfg.enabled?'on':''}" role="switch" aria-checked="${cfg.enabled?'true':'false'}" aria-label="Enable tracking"><i></i></button></div>
      <div class="evv-track-source-list">${rows}</div>
    </div>`;
    host.querySelectorAll('.evv-track-source-thumb img').forEach(img=>{
      const applyNaturalRatio=()=>{
        const w=Number(img.naturalWidth)||0,h=Number(img.naturalHeight)||0;
        if(!w||!h)return;
        const box=trackingThumbBox(w/h),wrap=img.parentElement;
        if(!wrap)return;
        wrap.style.width=box.width+'px';wrap.style.height=box.height+'px';
        wrap.style.aspectRatio=w+' / '+h;
      };
      if(img.complete)applyNaturalRatio();else img.addEventListener('load',applyNaturalRatio,{once:true});
    });
    const enable=host.querySelector('.evv-track-enable-switch');
    if(enable)enable.addEventListener('click',()=>{
      if(!candidateList.length){
        const msg='Tracking is unavailable: no other clip (background, layer, text, animation or effect) overlaps this clip in time. Add or move a clip so they overlap first.';
        if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast(msg,{icon:typeof toastIcons!=='undefined'?toastIcons.info:undefined,duration:3200});
        else if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast(msg,{duration:3200});
        return;
      }
      if(!cfg.targetId||!candidateList.some(item=>String(item.value)===String(cfg.targetId))){
        const msg='Choose a clip thumbnail above before enabling tracking.';
        if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast(msg,{icon:typeof toastIcons!=='undefined'?toastIcons.info:undefined,duration:2600});
        else if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast(msg,{duration:2600});
        return;
      }
      cfg.enabled=!cfg.enabled;
      if(cfg.enabled&&!cfg.targetBaseTransform)captureTrackingBase(cfg,cfg.targetId);
      if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();
      if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();
      renderTrackingPanel();
    });
    host.querySelectorAll('[data-track-source]').forEach(card=>card.addEventListener('click',()=>{
      const value=String(card.getAttribute('data-track-source')||'');
      const item=candidateList.find(candidate=>String(candidate.value)===value);
      if(!item)return;
      // Store the exact source ID carried by this thumbnail; display order/labels never
      // participate in tracking identity, so row reordering cannot mismatch the selection.
      cfg.targetId=item.value;captureTrackingBase(cfg,item.value);
      if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();
      if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();
      renderTrackingPanel();
    }));
  }
  window.ethvelleRenderCharacterTrackingPanel=renderTrackingPanel;
  window.ethvelleSetCharacterTracking=function(field,value,live){
    const ov=getTrackingOverlay();if(!ov)return;
    const cfg=ensureTrackingConfig(ov);
    if(field==='enabled'){
      cfg.enabled=!!value;
      if(cfg.enabled&&cfg.targetId&&!cfg.targetBaseTransform)captureTrackingBase(cfg,cfg.targetId);
    }else if(field==='followScale'||field==='followRotation')cfg[field]=!!value;
    else if(field==='targetId'){
      const valid=getTrackingCandidates(ov).some(g=>g.items.some(item=>item.value===String(value)));
      if(!valid)return;
      cfg.targetId=String(value);captureTrackingBase(cfg,cfg.targetId);
    }else if(field==='offsetX'||field==='offsetY')cfg[field]=clamp(value,-500,500);
    if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();
    if(!live&&typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();
    if(!live)renderTrackingPanel();
  };
  window.ethvellePersistCharacterTracking=function(){if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();};
  try{if(typeof panelContent!=='undefined')panelContent.characterTracking={title:'Clip Tracking',body:'<div class="evv-panel"></div>'};}catch(_e){}

  // Canvas transforms applied to a background are a source event too. Repaint its attached
  // media followers immediately rather than waiting for the next playback/render tick.
  const priorBgTrackingApply=window.applyBgTransform;
  if(typeof priorBgTrackingApply==='function'&&!priorBgTrackingApply.__evv71ClipTracking){
    const wrappedBgTrackingApply=function(){
      const result=priorBgTrackingApply.apply(this,arguments);
      try{
        const id=typeof selectedClip==='string'&&selectedClip.indexOf('bg')===0?selectedClip:'';
        if(id&&typeof window.ethvelleRefreshTrackingFollowersForSource==='function')window.ethvelleRefreshTrackingFollowersForSource('bg:'+id,trackingNow());
      }catch(_e){}
      return result;
    };
    wrappedBgTrackingApply.__evv71ClipTracking=true;window.applyBgTransform=wrappedBgTrackingApply;
  }

  function syncTrackingTool(){
    const row=$('bottomToolsClip');if(!row)return;
    let btn=$('clipTrackingTool');
    if(!btn){
      btn=document.createElement('div');btn.id='clipTrackingTool';btn.className='tool-item evv-context-tool';
      btn.setAttribute('role','button');btn.tabIndex=0;btn.title='Clip Tracking';
      btn.innerHTML='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.9" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><circle cx="8" cy="8" r="3"/><circle cx="17" cy="16" r="3"/><path d="M10.5 9.5l4 5M3 19c0-2.5 2-4 5-4m8-10c2.6 0 5 2 5 4"/></svg><span>Track</span>';
      btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{if(getTrackingOverlay())window.openPanel('characterTracking');});
      btn.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();btn.click();}});
      const before=$('clipForwardTool')||$('clipDuplicateTool')||null;
      if(before&&before.parentNode===row)row.insertBefore(btn,before);else row.appendChild(btn);
    }
    const ov=getTrackingOverlay();
    btn.classList.toggle('ev-v71-track-visible',!!ov);
    btn.setAttribute('aria-hidden',ov?'false':'true');
  }
  const priorOpenPanel=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof priorOpenPanel==='function'&&!priorOpenPanel.__evv71){
    const openWrapped=function(key){const r=priorOpenPanel.apply(this,arguments);if(key==='characterTracking')renderTrackingPanel();syncTrackingTool();return r;};
    openWrapped.__evv71=true;window.openPanel=openWrapped;
  }
  const priorSyncTools=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof priorSyncTools==='function'&&!priorSyncTools.__evv71){
    const syncWrapped=function(){const r=priorSyncTools.apply(this,arguments);syncTrackingTool();return r;};
    syncWrapped.__evv71=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=syncWrapped;
  }
  const priorSelect=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof priorSelect==='function'&&!priorSelect.__evv71){
    const selectWrapped=function(){const r=priorSelect.apply(this,arguments);syncTrackingTool();return r;};
    selectWrapped.__evv71=true;window.selectClipForChrome=selectWrapped;
  }

  /* ---------- Render one editor frame for image download or a new image clip ---------- */
  function frameCanvas(opts){
    const safeCapture=!!(opts&&opts.safe);
    const taintRisk=src=>{try{if(!src||(src.tagName!=='IMG'&&src.tagName!=='VIDEO'))return false;const u=String(src.currentSrc||src.src||'');return !src.crossOrigin&&/^https?:/i.test(u)&&u.indexOf(location.origin)!==0;}catch(_e){return false;}};
    if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();
    const stage=$('canvasStage'),stick=$('stickCanvas');
    const width=Math.max(1,Math.round(safeNum(typeof stageW!=='undefined'?stageW:0,0)||safeNum(stick&&stick.width,0)||640));
    const height=Math.max(1,Math.round(safeNum(typeof stageH!=='undefined'?stageH:0,0)||safeNum(stick&&stick.height,0)||360));
    const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=width;canvas.height=height;
    const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');if(!ctx)throw new Error('Canvas is not available');
    const bgModel=typeof currentBackgroundClip==='function'?currentBackgroundClip(safeNum(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)):null;
    ctx.fillStyle=stage?getComputedStyle(stage).backgroundColor:'#FFFFFF';ctx.fillRect(0,0,width,height);
    const bgImage=$('bgImgLayer');
    function drawContained(source,transform,mirrorX,mirrorY,adjustment,mask,enhanceAmount){
      if(!source)return false;
      if(safeCapture&&taintRisk(source))return false;
      const iw=source.videoWidth||source.naturalWidth||source.width||0;
      const ih=source.videoHeight||source.naturalHeight||source.height||0;
      if(!iw||!ih)return false;
      const fit=Math.min(width/iw,height/ih),dw=iw*fit,dh=ih*fit;
      const tr=Object.assign({scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0},transform||{});
      ctx.save();
      try{
        if(window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter)ctx.filter=window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter(adjustment,enhanceAmount||0)||'none';
        ctx.translate(width/2+safeNum(tr.offsetX),height/2+safeNum(tr.offsetY));
        if(safeNum(tr.rotation))ctx.rotate(safeNum(tr.rotation)*Math.PI/180);
        const scale=safeNum(tr.scale,1)||1;
        ctx.scale(scale*(mirrorX?-1:1),scale*(mirrorY?-1:1));
        ctx.translate(-width/2,-height/2);
        if(mask&&window.ethvelleApplyCanvasMaskClip)window.ethvelleApplyCanvasMaskClip(ctx,mask,width,height);
        ctx.drawImage(source,(width-dw)/2,(height-dh)/2,dw,dh);
        return true;
      }finally{ctx.restore();ctx.filter='none';}
    }
    if(bgImage&&bgImage.complete&&bgImage.naturalWidth){
      const bt=bgModel&&bgModel.transform?bgModel.transform:(typeof bgTransform!=='undefined'?bgTransform:null);
      drawContained(bgImage,bt,bgModel&&bgModel.mirrorX,bgModel&&bgModel.mirrorY,bgModel&&bgModel.adjustments,bgModel&&bgModel.visualMask,bgModel&&bgModel.enhanceAmount);
    }
    const now=safeNum(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec!=='undefined'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:0);
    const active=(Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).filter(o=>o&&(o.type==='image'||o.type==='video'||o.type==='text')&&now>=safeNum(o.trimStart)&&now<safeNum(o.trimEnd));
    active.forEach(ov=>{
      if(ov.type==='text'){
        const tr=Object.assign({scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0},(window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform?window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform(ov):null)||ov.transform||{});
        const size=Math.max(8,safeNum(ov.fontSize,36));
        ctx.save();
        try{
          ctx.translate(width/2+safeNum(tr.offsetX),height/2+safeNum(tr.offsetY));
          if(safeNum(tr.rotation))ctx.rotate(safeNum(tr.rotation)*Math.PI/180);
          ctx.scale(safeNum(tr.scale,1)||1,safeNum(tr.scale,1)||1);
          ctx.font=`${ov.fontStyle==='italic'?'italic ':''}${safeNum(ov.fontWeight,800)} ${size}px "${String(ov.fontFamily||'Inter').replace(/["\\]/g,'')}", sans-serif`;
          ctx.textAlign=ov.textAlign||'center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';
          ctx.fillStyle=ov.textColor||'#FFFFFF';
          ctx.globalAlpha=(clamp(ov.textOverallOpacity==null?100:ov.textOverallOpacity,0,100)/100)*(clamp(ov.textOpacity==null?100:ov.textOpacity,0,100)/100);
          const maxWidth=width*.82,lines=[];
          String(ov.textContent||'').split('\n').forEach(par=>{let line='';String(par).split(/\s+/).forEach(word=>{const test=line?line+' '+word:word;if(line&&ctx.measureText(test).width>maxWidth){lines.push(line);line=word;}else line=test;});lines.push(line);});
          const lh=size*1.2,total=lines.length*lh,strokeWidth=Math.max(0,safeNum(ov.strokeWidth));
          const pad=Math.max(0,safeNum(ov.textBackgroundPadding,7));
          const bgColor=ov.textBackgroundEnabled?ov.textBackgroundColor||'#182A66':null;
          const bgShape=String(ov.textBackgroundShape||'rounded');
          if(bgColor&&bgShape!=='rounded'&&typeof window.ethvelleDrawTextBackplate==='function'){
            const widest=Math.max(1,...lines.map(line=>Math.min(maxWidth,ctx.measureText(line).width)));
            const bx=(ov.textAlign||'center')==='left'?-maxWidth/2-pad:(ov.textAlign==='right'?maxWidth/2-widest-pad:-widest/2-pad);
            ctx.save();ctx.fillStyle=bgColor;ctx.globalAlpha=(clamp(ov.textOverallOpacity==null?100:ov.textOverallOpacity,0,100)/100)*(clamp(ov.textBackgroundOpacity==null?78:ov.textBackgroundOpacity,0,100)/100);
            window.ethvelleDrawTextBackplate(ctx,bx,-total/2-pad,widest+pad*2,total+pad*2,Number(ov.textBackgroundRadius)||8,bgShape);ctx.restore();
          }
          lines.forEach((line,i)=>{
            const y=-total/2+lh*(i+.5),lineWidth=Math.min(maxWidth,ctx.measureText(line).width);
            let x=0;if((ov.textAlign||'center')==='left')x=-maxWidth/2;else if(ov.textAlign==='right')x=maxWidth/2;
            if(bgColor&&bgShape==='rounded'){ctx.save();ctx.shadowColor='transparent';ctx.shadowBlur=0;ctx.fillStyle=bgColor;ctx.globalAlpha=(clamp(ov.textOverallOpacity==null?100:ov.textOverallOpacity,0,100)/100)*(clamp(ov.textBackgroundOpacity==null?78:ov.textBackgroundOpacity,0,100)/100);const bx=ov.textAlign==='left'?x-pad:ov.textAlign==='right'?x-lineWidth-pad:x-lineWidth/2-pad;ctx.fillRect(bx,y-lh/2-pad,lineWidth+pad*2,lh+pad*2);ctx.restore();ctx.globalAlpha=(clamp(ov.textOverallOpacity==null?100:ov.textOverallOpacity,0,100)/100)*(clamp(ov.textOpacity==null?100:ov.textOpacity,0,100)/100);ctx.fillStyle=ov.textColor||'#FFFFFF';}
            if(ov.shadowEnabled){ctx.shadowColor=ov.shadowColor||'#000000';ctx.shadowBlur=Math.max(0,safeNum(ov.shadowBlur));ctx.shadowOffsetX=safeNum(ov.shadowX);ctx.shadowOffsetY=Math.max(4,safeNum(ov.shadowY,6));}
            if(strokeWidth>0){ctx.lineWidth=strokeWidth*2;ctx.lineJoin='round';ctx.strokeStyle=(typeof ethvelleHexToRgba==='function')?ethvelleHexToRgba(ov.strokeColor||'#17172B',Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(ov.strokeOpacity==null?100:ov.strokeOpacity)))/100):(ov.strokeColor||'#17172B');if(window.ethvelleDrawStrokeDepth)window.ethvelleDrawStrokeDepth(ctx,line,x,y,maxWidth,strokeWidth,1);ctx.strokeText(line,x,y,maxWidth);}
            ctx.fillText(line,x,y,maxWidth);
          });
        }finally{ctx.restore();}
        return;
      }
      const el=(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined')?overlayMediaEls[ov.id]:null;
      let source=el;
      if(ov.type==='video'&&ov.chromaKey&&ov.chromaKey.enabled){
        const keyCanvas=(typeof overlayChromaCanvases!=='undefined')?overlayChromaCanvases[ov.id]:null;
        if(keyCanvas&&keyCanvas.width)source=keyCanvas;
      }
      if(!source)return;
      if(source.tagName==='VIDEO'&&source.readyState<2)return;
      const tr=window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform?window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform(ov):ov.transform;
      drawContained(source,tr,!!ov.mirrorX,!!ov.mirrorY,ov.adjustments,ov.visualMask,ov.enhanceAmount);
    });
    if(stick&&stick.width&&stick.height){try{ctx.drawImage(stick,0,0,width,height);}catch(_e){}}
    const fx=$('evcaV70FxCanvas');if(fx&&fx.width&&fx.height){try{ctx.drawImage(fx,0,0,width,height);}catch(_e){}}
    return canvas;
  }
  window.ethvelleFrameCanvas=frameCanvas;
  function captureDataUrl(){const canvas=frameCanvas();return canvas.toDataURL('image/png');}
  window.ethvelleSaveCanvasFrame=function(){
    try{
      const canvas=frameCanvas();
      const data=canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
      const a=document.createElement('a');
      a.href=data;a.download='Ethvelle-Frame-'+Math.max(0,safeNum(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)).toFixed(2).replace('.','-')+'s.png';
      document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();
      if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Frame saved to device',{icon:typeof toastIcons!=='undefined'?toastIcons.check:undefined,duration:1500});
    }catch(err){console.error('Ethvelle frame capture failed',err);if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not save this frame. A source image or video may block canvas capture.',{duration:2800});}
  };
  // v299: capture the current canvas frame and add it to the timeline as a new Background clip.
  window.ethvelleAddCanvasFrameAsBackground=function(){
    try{
      const data=captureDataUrl();
      if(typeof addBackgroundClip!=='function')throw new Error('Background function is unavailable');
      addBackgroundClip('url('+data+')');
      if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Current frame added as Background',{icon:typeof toastIcons!=='undefined'?toastIcons.check:undefined,duration:1600});
    }catch(err){console.error('Ethvelle frame background failed',err);if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not add this frame as a Background. A source image or video may block canvas capture.',{duration:2800});}
  };
  window.ethvelleAddCanvasFrameAsLayer=function(){
    try{
      const data=captureDataUrl();
      if(typeof addMediaOverlay!=='function')throw new Error('Image layer function is unavailable');
      addMediaOverlay({type:'image',src:data,name:'Captured Frame',duration:3,thumbs:[data]});
      if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Current frame added as a new image layer',{duration:1600});
    }catch(err){console.error('Ethvelle frame layer failed',err);if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not add this frame as a layer. A source image or video may block canvas capture.',{duration:2800});}
  };

  /* ---------- Zoom overlay controls ---------- */
  function installZoomFrameButtons(){
    const hud=$('evcaInlineZoomHud');if(!hud)return;
    const out=$('evcaZoomOut');
    if(out){
      if(out.dataset.evV72BackIcon!=='1'){
        out.innerHTML='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M14 5 7 12l7 7M20 5l-7 7 7 7"/></svg>';
        out.dataset.evV72BackIcon='1';
      }
      out.setAttribute('aria-label','Back to normal canvas');out.title='Back to canvas';
    }
    if(!$('evcaZoomSave')){
      const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.id='evcaZoomSave';b.className='evca-v71-frame-btn';b.title='Save current frame to device';b.setAttribute('aria-label','Save current frame to device');b.textContent='↓';
      b.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();window.ethvelleSaveCanvasFrame();});hud.appendChild(b);
    }
    if(!$('evcaZoomLayer')){
      const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.id='evcaZoomLayer';b.className='evca-v71-frame-btn';b.title='Add current frame as a new layer';b.setAttribute('aria-label','Add current frame as a new layer');b.textContent='＋';
      b.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();window.ethvelleAddCanvasFrameAsLayer();});hud.appendChild(b);
    }
  }
  const priorZoomToggle=window.toggleCanvasZoomView;
  if(typeof priorZoomToggle==='function'&&!priorZoomToggle.__evv71){
    const zoomWrapped=function(){const r=priorZoomToggle.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(installZoomFrameButtons);return r;};
    zoomWrapped.__evv71=true;window.toggleCanvasZoomView=zoomWrapped;
  }
  /* No subtree MutationObserver here: installZoomFrameButtons mutates the HUD itself,
     so observing the whole editor made its own writes trigger an endless observer loop. */
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{syncTrackingTool();installZoomFrameButtons();},{once:true});
  else{syncTrackingTool();installZoomFrameButtons();}
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v72-deep-fixes-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const n=(v,f=0)=>{const x=Number(v);return Number.isFinite(x)?x:f;};

  /* ---------- Gesture-safe video preview ---------- */
  function attemptVideoPlay(video,allowMutedFallback=true){
    if(!video||video.tagName!=='VIDEO')return Promise.resolve(false);
    const ov=(Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).find(item=>item&&String(item.id)===String(video.dataset.evV72OverlayId||''));
    if(typeof window.evSafePlayOverlayVideo==='function')return window.evSafePlayOverlayVideo(video,ov);
    return Promise.resolve(false);
  }
  window.ethvelleV72PlayVideo=attemptVideoPlay;

  function primeActiveVideosFromGesture(){
    if(typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&isPlaying)return;
    let now=n(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec!=='undefined'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:0);
    // If a video clip is selected but the playhead is outside that clip, begin at its start.
    // This prevents a selected video later on the timeline from appearing frozen/blank when
    // the user presses Play without first scrubbing into the clip.
    const selectedVideo=(Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).find(o=>o&&o.type==='video'&&String(o.id)===String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'?selectedClip:''));
    if(selectedVideo&&(now<n(selectedVideo.trimStart)||now>=n(selectedVideo.trimEnd))){
      try{setCurrentAbsoluteTime(n(selectedVideo.trimStart));now=n(selectedVideo.trimStart);}catch(_e){}
    }
    try{if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle video pre-render failed',err);}
    // Ensure the video overlay layer has synchronously created the active <video> elements
    // before calling play() in the trusted button gesture.
    try{if(typeof renderOverlayMedia==='function')renderOverlayMedia(now);}catch(_e){}
    const active=(Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).filter(o=>o&&o.type==='video'&&now>=n(o.trimStart)&&now<n(o.trimEnd));
    active.forEach(ov=>{
      let el=null;try{el=typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'?overlayMediaEls[ov.id]:null;}catch(_e){}
      if(!el||el.tagName!=='VIDEO')return;
      const speed=n(ov.speed,1)||1;
      const max=Math.max(.05,n(ov.sourceDuration,el.duration||n(ov.trimEnd)-n(ov.trimStart))-.02);
      const local=Math.max(0,Math.min(max,n(ov.sourceOffset)+(now-n(ov.trimStart))*speed));
      try{if(el.readyState>=1&&Number.isFinite(local)&&Math.abs(el.currentTime-local)>.04){el.muted=true;el.pause();el.currentTime=local;}}catch(_e){}
      el.playsInline=true;el.autoplay=false;el.setAttribute('playsinline','');el.setAttribute('webkit-playsinline','');el.removeAttribute('autoplay');el.preload='auto';
      const retryWhenReady=()=>{if(typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&isPlaying)attemptVideoPlay(el,true);};
      el.addEventListener('loadedmetadata',retryWhenReady,{once:true});
      el.addEventListener('loadeddata',retryWhenReady,{once:true});
      el.addEventListener('canplay',retryWhenReady,{once:true});
      // The trusted gesture may pre-roll muted; evSafePlayVideo will not unmute until
      // playback is active, the cut gate is clear and source position is within 40ms.
      attemptVideoPlay(el,true);
    });
  }
  window.ethvelleV72PrimeVideoPlayback=primeActiveVideosFromGesture;
  const originalTogglePlayback=window.togglePlayback;
  if(typeof originalTogglePlayback==='function'&&!originalTogglePlayback.__evv73VideoGesture){
    const gestureToggle=function(){if(!isPlaying)primeActiveVideosFromGesture();return originalTogglePlayback.apply(this,arguments);};
    gestureToggle.__evv73VideoGesture=true;window.togglePlayback=gestureToggle;
  }
  const playButton=$('playBtn');
  if(playButton&&!playButton.dataset.evV72PrimeBound){
    playButton.dataset.evV72PrimeBound='1';
    playButton.addEventListener('pointerdown',primeActiveVideosFromGesture,{capture:true,passive:true});
    playButton.addEventListener('click',primeActiveVideosFromGesture,{capture:true});
  }
  // Retry when a newly created media element finishes decoding after playback has started.
  const mediaLayer=$('overlayMediaLayer');
  if(mediaLayer&&!mediaLayer.dataset.evV72CanplayObserver){
    mediaLayer.dataset.evV72CanplayObserver='1';
    const retryActiveVideo=e=>{
      const v=e.target;
      if(v&&v.tagName==='VIDEO'&&v.dataset.evWarmElement!=='1'&&typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&isPlaying)attemptVideoPlay(v,true);
    };
    mediaLayer.addEventListener('loadeddata',retryActiveVideo,true);
    mediaLayer.addEventListener('canplay',retryActiveVideo,true);
  }

  /* ---------- Frame capture directly in the bottom options row ---------- */
  function makeFrameTool(id,label,title,icon,handler){
    const item=document.createElement('div');item.id=id;item.className='tool-item evv72-frame-tool';
    item.setAttribute('role','button');item.tabIndex=0;item.title=title;item.setAttribute('aria-label',title);
    item.innerHTML=icon+'<span>'+label+'</span>';
    const run=e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();try{handler();}catch(err){console.error('Frame toolbar action failed',err);}};
    item.addEventListener('click',run);item.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){run(e);}});
    return item;
  }
  const downloadIcon='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 3v12m-5-5 5 5 5-5M4 20h16"/></svg>';
  const layerIcon='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><rect x="3.5" y="3.5" width="17" height="17" rx="2"/><path d="M12 7.5v9M7.5 12h9"/></svg>';
  function ensureFrameToolbarButtons(){
    [['bottomToolsDefault','default'],['bottomToolsClip','clip'],['bottomToolsKeyframe','keyframe']].forEach(([hostId,suffix])=>{
      const row=$(hostId);if(!row)return;
      const saveId='evv72SaveFrame-'+suffix,layerId='evv72AddFrame-'+suffix;
      const anchor=suffix==='default'?row.querySelector('[data-tool=\"model\"]'):suffix==='clip'?$('clipSplitTool'):row.children[1];
      let save=$(saveId),layer=$(layerId);
      if(!save)save=makeFrameTool(saveId,'Save PNG','Save current canvas frame as a PNG file',downloadIcon,()=>window.ethvelleSaveCanvasFrame&&window.ethvelleSaveCanvasFrame());
      if(!layer)layer=makeFrameTool(layerId,'Add Layer','Add the current canvas frame as a new image layer',layerIcon,()=>window.ethvelleAddCanvasFrameAsLayer&&window.ethvelleAddCanvasFrameAsLayer());
      if(anchor&&anchor.parentNode===row){row.insertBefore(save,anchor);row.insertBefore(layer,anchor);}
      else{if(save.parentNode!==row)row.appendChild(save);if(layer.parentNode!==row)row.appendChild(layer);}
    });
  }
  const priorSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof priorSync==='function'&&!priorSync.__evv72FrameTools){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=priorSync.apply(this,arguments);ensureFrameToolbarButtons();return r;};
    wrapped.__evv72FrameTools=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=wrapped;
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',ensureFrameToolbarButtons,{once:true});
  else ensureFrameToolbarButtons();

})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v76-recording-fixes-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const q=(root,sel)=>root?root.querySelector(sel):null;
  const qa=(root,sel)=>root?[...root.querySelectorAll(sel)]:[];
  const screen=$('screen-editor');

  /* ---------- Single Frame Capture sheet ---------- */
  function ensureFrameSheet(){
    if($('evcaFrameCaptureSheet'))return $('evcaFrameCaptureSheet');
    if(!screen)return null;
    const sheet=document.createElement('div');
    sheet.id='evcaFrameCaptureSheet';
    sheet.innerHTML=`
      <div class="evca-frame-sheet" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Frame Capture">
        <div class="evca-frame-sheet-head"><span>Frame Capture</span><button type="button" class="evca-frame-sheet-close" aria-label="Close"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6 6l12 12M18 6 6 18"/></svg></button></div>
        <button type="button" class="evca-frame-action" data-frame-action="layer">
          <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><rect x="3.5" y="3.5" width="17" height="17" rx="2"/><path d="M12 7.5v9M7.5 12h9"/></svg>
          <span><strong>Add current frame as a layer</strong><small>Insert the captured frame into the timeline as an image</small></span>
        </button>
        <button type="button" class="evca-frame-action" data-frame-action="background">
          <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><rect x="3" y="4" width="18" height="16" rx="2"/><path d="M3 15l5-5 4 4 3-3 6 6"/><circle cx="8.5" cy="8.5" r="1.3"/></svg>
          <span><strong>Capture frame as Background</strong><small>Add the current canvas frame as a new background clip</small></span>
        </button>
        <button type="button" class="evca-frame-action" data-frame-action="save">
          <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 3v12m-5-5 5 5 5-5M4 20h16"/></svg>
          <span><strong>Save frame to device</strong><small>Download the current canvas frame as a PNG image</small></span>
        </button>
      </div>`;
    sheet.addEventListener('click',e=>{
      if(e.target===sheet||e.target.closest('.evca-frame-sheet-close')){closeFrameSheet();return;}
      const action=e.target.closest('[data-frame-action]')?.dataset.frameAction;
      if(!action)return;
      closeFrameSheet();
      try{
        if(action==='layer'&&typeof window.ethvelleAddCanvasFrameAsLayer==='function')window.ethvelleAddCanvasFrameAsLayer();
        else if(action==='background'&&typeof window.ethvelleAddCanvasFrameAsBackground==='function')window.ethvelleAddCanvasFrameAsBackground();
        else if(action==='save'&&typeof window.ethvelleSaveCanvasFrame==='function')window.ethvelleSaveCanvasFrame();
        else if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast('Frame capture is not ready yet',{duration:1600});
      }catch(err){console.error('Frame capture action failed',err);}
    });
    screen.appendChild(sheet);
    return sheet;
  }
  function openFrameSheet(){const sheet=ensureFrameSheet();if(sheet){sheet.classList.add('active');sheet.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');}}
  function closeFrameSheet(){const sheet=$('evcaFrameCaptureSheet');if(sheet){sheet.classList.remove('active');sheet.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');}}
  window.openEthvelleFrameCapture=openFrameSheet;
  window.closeEthvelleFrameCapture=closeFrameSheet;

  const frameIcon='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><rect x="3" y="3" width="18" height="18" rx="2"/><path d="M8 8h8v8H8zM12 2v3m0 14v3M2 12h3m14 0h3"/></svg>';
  function replaceFrameToolbarItems(){
    [['bottomToolsDefault','default'],['bottomToolsClip','clip'],['bottomToolsKeyframe','keyframe']].forEach(([hostId,kind])=>{
      const host=$(hostId);if(!host)return;
      // Remove a legacy button once, then reuse the replacement on every later toolbar sync.
      qa(host,'.evv72-frame-tool').forEach(el=>el.remove());
      let btn=$('evcaFrameCaptureTool-'+kind);
      if(!btn){
        btn=document.createElement('div');
        btn.className='tool-item evca-frame-capture-tool';
        btn.id='evcaFrameCaptureTool-'+kind;
        btn.setAttribute('role','button');btn.tabIndex=0;
        btn.title='Capture the current canvas frame';
        btn.setAttribute('aria-label','Frame Capture');
        btn.innerHTML=frameIcon+'<span>Frame</span>';
        btn.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();openFrameSheet();});
        btn.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();openFrameSheet();}});
      }
      // Keep exactly one node per row; do not tear down the row during frequent selection syncs.
      qa(host,'.evca-frame-capture-tool').forEach(el=>{if(el!==btn)el.remove();});
      const anchor=kind==='default'?q(host,'[data-tool="media"]'):kind==='clip'?$ ('clipMediaAccessTool'):$('keyframeMediaAccessTool');
      if(anchor&&anchor.parentNode===host){if(btn.previousElementSibling!==anchor)anchor.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',btn);}
      else if(btn.parentNode!==host)host.appendChild(btn);
    });
  }
  const oldSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof oldSync==='function'&&!oldSync.__evv75FrameCapture){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=oldSync.apply(this,arguments);replaceFrameToolbarItems();return result;};
    wrapped.__evv75FrameCapture=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=wrapped;
  }
  replaceFrameToolbarItems();

  /* Keep the single transport-row zoom button visible regardless of selected layer type. */
  function syncCanvasZoomEntry(){
    const wrap=q(screen,'.canvas-wrap'),button=$('transportZoomBtn');
    if(!button)return;
    const active=!!(wrap?.classList.contains('evca-inline-zoom')||screen?.classList.contains('evca-inline-zoom-active'));
    button.style.display='inline-flex';
    button.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(active));
    button.title=active?'Exit canvas zoom':'Zoom canvas';
  }
  const oldToggle=window.toggleCanvasZoomView;
  if(typeof oldToggle==='function'&&!oldToggle.__evv75Zoom){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=oldToggle.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(()=>{syncCanvasZoomEntry();ensureSingleZoomFrameButton();});return result;};
    wrapped.__evv75Zoom=true;window.toggleCanvasZoomView=wrapped;
  }
  function ensureSingleZoomFrameButton(){
    const hud=$('evcaInlineZoomHud');if(!hud)return;
    // v71 used to add two separate Save and Layer buttons. Replace them with one shared entry.
    ['evcaZoomSave','evcaZoomLayer'].forEach(id=>$(id)?.remove());
    let button=$('evcaZoomFrameCapture');
    if(!button){
      button=document.createElement('button');button.type='button';button.id='evcaZoomFrameCapture';
      button.className='evca-zoom-frame-open';button.title='Frame Capture';button.setAttribute('aria-label','Frame Capture');
      button.innerHTML=frameIcon;
      button.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();openFrameSheet();});
    }
    const controlBar=hud.querySelector('.evca-zoom-control-bar');
    if(controlBar&&button.parentNode!==controlBar)controlBar.appendChild(button);
  }
  const oldOpen=window.openCustomAnimationStudio,oldClose=window.closeCustomAnimationStudio;
  if(typeof oldOpen==='function'&&!oldOpen.__evv75ZoomSync){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldOpen.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(()=>{syncCanvasZoomEntry();ensureSingleZoomFrameButton();});return r;};
    wrapped.__evv75ZoomSync=true;window.openCustomAnimationStudio=wrapped;
  }
  if(typeof oldClose==='function'&&!oldClose.__evv75ZoomSync){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldClose.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(syncCanvasZoomEntry);return r;};
    wrapped.__evv75ZoomSync=true;window.closeCustomAnimationStudio=wrapped;
  }
  syncCanvasZoomEntry();

  /* ---------- Selection follows the clip that passes under the fixed playhead ---------- */
  function currentSelectedRef(){
    try{return typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?String(selectedClip):'';}catch(_e){return '';}
  }
  function selectedElementForRef(ref){
    const root=$('timelineInner');if(!root||!ref)return null;
    // A selected clip of a NON-active character (readonly strip) must resolve to ITS OWN row,
    // otherwise the active character (the fox) steals the selection while sliding.
    const ownerRaw=window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionCharacterIndex;
    if(ownerRaw!=null&&Number.isInteger(Number(ownerRaw))&&Number(ownerRaw)!==Number(activeCharIdx)&&(ref==='main'||/^seg\d+$/.test(ref))){
      const own=root.querySelector('.char-row[data-char-idx="'+Number(ownerRaw)+'"] .anim-clip-readonly[data-readonly-clip-ref="'+ref+'"]');
      if(own)return own;
    }
    if(ref==='main')return $('animClipEl');
    if(ref.startsWith('seg'))return root.querySelector('.anim-clip[data-seg-idx="'+ref.slice(3)+'"]');
    const overlay=qa(root,'.overlay-clip[data-overlay-id]').find(el=>String(el.dataset.overlayId)===ref);
    if(overlay)return overlay;
    const bg=qa(root,'.track-clip[data-bg-id]').find(el=>String(el.dataset.bgId)===ref);
    if(bg)return bg;
    const char=ref.match(/^char(\d+)$/);
    if(char)return root.querySelector('.char-row[data-char-idx="'+char[1]+'"]');
    return null;
  }
  function refForClip(el){
    if(!el)return null;
    if(el.id==='animClipEl')return 'main';
    if(el.classList.contains('anim-clip-readonly')&&el.dataset.readonlyClipRef!=null)return String(el.dataset.readonlyClipRef);
    if(el.classList.contains('anim-clip')&&el.dataset.segIdx!=null)return 'seg'+el.dataset.segIdx;
    if(el.classList.contains('overlay-clip')&&el.dataset.overlayId!=null)return String(el.dataset.overlayId);
    if(el.classList.contains('track-clip')&&el.dataset.bgId!=null)return String(el.dataset.bgId);
    return null;
  }
  function rowForPointer(x,y){
    const hit=document.elementFromPoint(x,y);
    return hit&&hit.closest?hit.closest('.overlay-track-row,#backgroundRow,.char-row'):null;
  }
  function timelineEntriesForRow(row){
    if(!row)return [];
    if(row.id==='backgroundRow'){
      return (Array.isArray(backgrounds)?backgrounds:[]).map(model=>({id:String(model.id),model,characterIndex:null,kind:'background'}));
    }
    if(row.classList.contains('overlay-track-row')){
      const group=String(row.dataset.group||'clips');
      return (Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).filter(model=>group==='audio'?model.type==='audio':group==='text'?model.type==='text':model.type!=='audio'&&model.type!=='text')
        .map(model=>({id:String(model.id),model,characterIndex:null,kind:'overlay'}));
    }
    if(row.classList.contains('char-row')){
      const idx=Number(row.dataset.charIdx);
      if(!Number.isInteger(idx)||!Array.isArray(characters)||!characters[idx])return [];
      const model=characters[idx];
      const clip=idx===Number(activeCharIdx)?activeClip:model.clip;
      const segments=idx===Number(activeCharIdx)?splitStickmanSegments:model.segments;
      if(typeof getAllStickmanClips!=='function')return [];
      return getAllStickmanClips(clip,segments).map(entry=>({id:String(entry.ref),model:entry.clip,characterIndex:idx,kind:'character'}));
    }
    return [];
  }
  function timelineEntryElement(row,entry){
    if(!row||!entry)return null;
    if(entry.kind==='background')return qa(row,'.track-clip[data-bg-id]').find(el=>String(el.dataset.bgId)===entry.id)||null;
    if(entry.kind==='overlay')return qa(row,'.overlay-clip[data-overlay-id]').find(el=>String(el.dataset.overlayId)===entry.id)||null;
    if(entry.kind==='character'){
      return qa(row,'.anim-clip,.anim-clip-readonly').find(el=>{
        const ref=el.dataset.readonlyClipRef!=null?String(el.dataset.readonlyClipRef):(el.id==='animClipEl'?'main':el.dataset.segIdx!=null?'seg'+el.dataset.segIdx:'');
        return ref===entry.id;
      })||null;
    }
    return null;
  }
  function resolveTimelineClipAtTime(row,x,y){
    if(!row)return null;
    const now=typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?Number(getCurrentAbsoluteTime()):Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
    const entries=timelineEntriesForRow(row).filter(entry=>{
      const start=Number(entry.model&&entry.model.trimStart)||0;
      const end=Number(entry.model&&entry.model.trimEnd)||start;
      return end>start&&now>=start-0.000001&&now<end-0.000001;
    });
    if(!entries.length)return null;
    if(entries.length===1)return {id:entries[0].id,characterIndex:entries[0].characterIndex};
    // v258: stacked clips (x on top, y underneath, same time coverage). If the clip that is
    // already selected still covers the playhead, it stays selected for the whole slide —
    // the border never jumps to the other clip. Only when the selected clip no longer covers
    // the playhead does the pointer's lane pick a new one.
    const keepRef=currentSelectedRef();
    if(keepRef){
      const keepOwner=window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionCharacterIndex;
      const keepEntry=entries.find(entry=>String(entry.id)===keepRef&&(entry.characterIndex==null||keepOwner==null||String(entry.characterIndex)===String(keepOwner)));
      if(keepEntry)return {id:keepEntry.id,characterIndex:keepEntry.characterIndex};
    }
    // Multiple clips can occupy separate vertical sub-lanes at the same time. Use the
    // pointer's lane only to choose between those data-matched candidates; time membership
    // always comes from trimStart/trimEnd, never horizontal DOM rectangles.
    const hit=document.elementFromPoint(x,y);
    const hitClip=hit&&hit.closest?hit.closest('.anim-clip,.anim-clip-readonly,.overlay-clip,.track-clip[data-bg-id]'):null;
    const hitTop=hitClip?Number.parseFloat(hitClip.style.top||'0'):NaN;
    let best=entries[0],bestScore=Infinity;
    entries.forEach(entry=>{
      const el=timelineEntryElement(row,entry);
      const top=el?Number.parseFloat(el.style.top||'0'):NaN;
      const score=Number.isFinite(hitTop)&&Number.isFinite(top)?Math.abs(top-hitTop):0;
      if(score<bestScore){best=entry;bestScore=score;}
    });
    return {id:best.id,characterIndex:best.characterIndex};
  }
  window.__ethvelleResolveTimelineClipAtTime=resolveTimelineClipAtTime;
  function autoSelectAtPlayhead(){
    if(typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&isPlaying)return;
    // A clip's own move/trim gesture owns selection until release. The only exception
    // is an actual playhead scrub, where the lock must yield to the clip under the playhead.
    const selectionLock=String(window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionLockId||'');
    if(!window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing){
      const activeManipulator=document.querySelector('#timelineInner .dragging,#timelineInner [data-ev-timeline-drag-active="1"]');
      if(selectionLock||activeManipulator)return;
    }
    const ref=currentSelectedRef();
    const pointer=window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionPointer;
    // A quick tap on a clip selects that clip but must not immediately be replaced by the
    // clip currently beneath the playhead. Auto-selection is reserved for real scrub drags.
    if(!window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing&&pointer&&!pointer.moved)return;
    // Stay in the row where the slide STARTED; a finger drifting over the fox's row must not switch rows.
    const pointerRow=pointer&&pointer.active?((pointer.startRow&&pointer.startRow.isConnected)?pointer.startRow:rowForPointer(pointer.x,pointer.y)):null;
    const selected=selectedElementForRef(ref);
    let row=pointerRow||selected?.closest('.layer-row')||null;
    if(!row&&ref){
      const char=ref.match(/^char(\d+)$/);
      if(char)row=$('timelineInner')?.querySelector('.char-row[data-char-idx="'+char[1]+'"]')||null;
    }
    if(!row)return;
    const x=pointer&&pointer.active?pointer.x:0;
    const y=pointer&&pointer.active?pointer.y:0;
    const result=resolveTimelineClipAtTime(row,x,y);
    if(!result||String(result.id)===String(ref)&&String(result.characterIndex??'')===String(window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionCharacterIndex??''))return;
    if(typeof window.__ethvelleLightSelectTimelineClip==='function')window.__ethvelleLightSelectTimelineClip(result.id,result.characterIndex);
  }
  let selectRaf=0,forceSelectPending=false;
  function scheduleAutoSelect(force=false){
    // Selection is cheap while a gesture is live: resolve clip membership from model time
    // once per display frame and paint only the selection chrome. Heavy toolbar/keyframe/media
    // synchronization is deferred to the single pointer-up commit.
    if(force)forceSelectPending=true;
    if(selectRaf)return;
    selectRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
      selectRaf=0;
      autoSelectAtPlayhead();
      if(forceSelectPending){
        forceSelectPending=false;
        if(typeof window.__ethvelleCommitTimelineSelection==='function')window.__ethvelleCommitTimelineSelection();
        if(window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionPointer)window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionPointer.active=false;
      }
    });
  }
  const timeline=$('timelineScroll');
  if(timeline&&!timeline.dataset.evcaV74AutoSelect){
    timeline.dataset.evcaV74AutoSelect='1';
    let lastLeft=timeline.scrollLeft;
    timeline.addEventListener('scroll',()=>{
      const left=timeline.scrollLeft;
      if(Math.abs(left-lastLeft)>.25){lastLeft=left;scheduleAutoSelect(false);}
    },{passive:true});
    // Commit selection immediately after a gesture; during movement the expensive row scan
    // is throttled because the playhead/canvas remain independently frame-smooth.
    timeline.addEventListener('pointerup',()=>scheduleAutoSelect(true),{passive:true});
    timeline.addEventListener('touchend',()=>scheduleAutoSelect(true),{passive:true});
  }
  // Scrubbing the playhead also changes the clip crossing the fixed centre line.
  const oldSetTime=window.setCurrentAbsoluteTime;
  if(typeof oldSetTime==='function'&&!oldSetTime.__evcaV75AutoSelect){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldSetTime.apply(this,arguments);scheduleAutoSelect();return r;};
    wrapped.__evcaV75AutoSelect=true;window.setCurrentAbsoluteTime=wrapped;
  }

  /* Force sharp character-row corners after each dynamic timeline render. This is style-only:
     the observer watches child replacement, not style attributes, so it cannot feed back. */
  function forceSquareCharacterRows(){
    const rows=$('characterRows');if(!rows)return;
    qa(rows,'.char-row,.char-row .char-row-track,.char-row .char-row-track *').forEach(el=>{
      el.style.setProperty('border-radius','0','important');
    });
  }
  const oldRenderChars=window.renderCharacterLayers;
  if(typeof oldRenderChars==='function'&&!oldRenderChars.__evcaV75Square){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldRenderChars.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(forceSquareCharacterRows);return r;};
    wrapped.__evcaV75Square=true;window.renderCharacterLayers=wrapped;
  }
  const oldRenderClips=window.renderTimelineClip;
  if(typeof oldRenderClips==='function'&&!oldRenderClips.__evcaV75Square){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldRenderClips.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(forceSquareCharacterRows);scheduleAutoSelect();return r;};
    wrapped.__evcaV75Square=true;window.renderTimelineClip=wrapped;
  }
  forceSquareCharacterRows();

  // The older v72 script also installs a DOMContentLoaded callback that may recreate
  // Save PNG + Add Layer after this file has already inserted the single Frame button.
  // Run this normalizer after that older callback so the final live toolbar always has one.
  function normalizeV75UI(){
    replaceFrameToolbarItems();
    syncCanvasZoomEntry();
    ensureSingleZoomFrameButton();
    forceSquareCharacterRows();
    scheduleAutoSelect();
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',normalizeV75UI,{once:true});
  else normalizeV75UI();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v77-editor-fixes-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);

  /* The zoom-out control already exits zoom through its original handler. Upgrade
     only its placeholder glyph, leaving the control's behavior and layout untouched. */
  function cleanZoomHud(){
    const back=$('evcaZoomOut');
    if(!back)return;
    const restoreIcon='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.9" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 3v5H3M3 3l6 6"/><path d="M16 3v5h5M21 3l-6 6"/><path d="M3 16v5h5M3 21l6-6"/><path d="M21 16v5h-5M21 21l-6-6"/></svg>';
    const currentSvg=back.querySelector('svg');
    const iconIsCurrent=!!currentSvg&&currentSvg.getAttribute('viewBox')==='0 0 24 24'&&
      currentSvg.querySelector('path')?.getAttribute('d')==='M8 3v5H3M3 3l6 6'&&currentSvg.querySelectorAll('path').length===4;
    if(!iconIsCurrent)back.innerHTML=restoreIcon;
    back.setAttribute('aria-label','Back to original canvas');
    back.title='Back to original canvas';
  }

  function attachZoomHudObserver(){
    const hud=$('evcaInlineZoomHud');
    if(!hud||typeof MutationObserver!=='function'||hud.dataset.evV77IconObserver==='1')return;
    hud.dataset.evV77IconObserver='1';
    new MutationObserver(cleanZoomHud).observe(hud,{childList:true,subtree:true});
    cleanZoomHud();
  }
  if(typeof MutationObserver==='function'&&document.body){
    new MutationObserver(attachZoomHudObserver).observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  }
  attachZoomHudObserver();
  cleanZoomHud();

  const oldToggle=window.toggleCanvasZoomView;
  if(typeof oldToggle==='function'&&!oldToggle.__evv77ZoomIcon){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const result=oldToggle.apply(this,arguments);
      requestAnimationFrame(cleanZoomHud);
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evv77ZoomIcon=true;
    window.toggleCanvasZoomView=wrapped;
  }
})();
</script>
