<script id="ethvelle-v80-final-fixes-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const getSelectedId=()=>String(typeof selectedClip==='undefined'||selectedClip==null?'':selectedClip);
  const getOverlay=id=>{try{const list=(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays))?overlays:(Array.isArray(window.overlays)?window.overlays:[]);return list.find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===String(id))||null;}catch(_e){return null;}};
  const getObjectClip=id=>typeof window.ethvelleObjectClipById==='function'?window.ethvelleObjectClipById(id):null;

  // Recalculate the two swatch systems after a toggle or color change.
  window.ethvelleRefreshTextSwatches=function(){
    const root=$('ethvelleTextStylePanel');if(!root)return;
    const id=getSelectedId(),model=getOverlay(id);if(!model)return;
    const enabled={strokeColor:Number(model.strokeWidth)>0,shadowColor:!!model.shadowEnabled,textBackgroundColor:!!model.textBackgroundEnabled};
    root.querySelectorAll('.evts-swatches[data-swatch-key]').forEach(group=>{
      const key=group.dataset.swatchKey,allow=enabled[key]!==false,value=String(model[key]||'').toLowerCase();
      group.querySelectorAll('.evts-swatch[data-color]').forEach(btn=>btn.classList.toggle('active',allow&&String(btn.dataset.color||'').toLowerCase()===value));
    });
  };
  const oldStyleChanged=window.ethvelleTextStyleChanged;
  if(typeof oldStyleChanged==='function'&&!oldStyleChanged.__evv80Swatches){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldStyleChanged.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(()=>{try{window.ethvelleRefreshTextSwatches();if(window.ethvelleRefreshDockSwatches)window.ethvelleRefreshDockSwatches();}catch(_e){}});return r;};
    wrapped.__evv80Swatches=true;window.ethvelleTextStyleChanged=wrapped;
  }
  const oldStylePanel=window.openEthvelleTextStylePanel;
  if(typeof oldStylePanel==='function'&&!oldStylePanel.__evv80Swatches){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldStylePanel.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(()=>window.ethvelleRefreshTextSwatches());return r;};
    wrapped.__evv80Swatches=true;window.openEthvelleTextStylePanel=wrapped;
  }

  // Restore the object Key button for all keyframe-capable clips, including text.
  function setDisplayStable(el,value){
    if(!el)return;
    if(el.style.getPropertyValue('display')!==value||el.style.getPropertyPriority('display')!=='important')el.style.setProperty('display',value,'important');
  }
  function stabilizeBottomTools(){
    const screen=$('screen-editor'),clipRow=$('bottomToolsClip'),defaultRow=$('bottomToolsDefault'),keyRow=$('bottomToolsKeyframe');
    if(!screen||!clipRow||!defaultRow||!keyRow)return;
    const id=getSelectedId(),kfSelected=typeof selectedKfIdx!=='undefined'&&selectedKfIdx!==null;
    if(kfSelected){setDisplayStable(defaultRow,'none');setDisplayStable(clipRow,'none');setDisplayStable(keyRow,'flex');return;}
    setDisplayStable(keyRow,'none');
    if(!id){setDisplayStable(clipRow,'none');setDisplayStable(defaultRow,'flex');return;}
    setDisplayStable(defaultRow,'none');setDisplayStable(clipRow,'flex');
    const ov=getOverlay(id),bg=Array.isArray(window.backgrounds)?window.backgrounds.find(b=>b&&String(b.id)===id):null;
    const obj=getObjectClip(id),isChar=id==='main'||/^seg\d+$/.test(id),isText=!!ov&&ov.type==='text';
    const key=$('clipKeyTool');setDisplayStable(key,(isChar||!!obj)?'flex':'none');
    const anim=$('clipOverlayAnimationTool');setDisplayStable(anim,((!!ov&&['image','video','text'].includes(ov.type))||isChar)?'flex':'none');
    const filter=$('clipOverlayFilterTool');setDisplayStable(filter,(!!ov&&['image','video','text'].includes(ov.type))||!!bg?'flex':'none');
    ['clipMirrorTool','clipMaskTool','clipEffectsTool'].forEach(toolId=>setDisplayStable($(toolId),(!!ov&&['image','video','text'].includes(ov.type))||!!bg||isChar?'flex':'none'));
    if(isText){
      // Selecting a text clip must behave like selecting any other object layer: keep the
      // shared Key button and object Clip Animations tool, but do not inject six extra text
      // styling actions into the normal bottom row. Text editing opens only from its own entry.
      setDisplayStable($('clipTextEditTool'),'flex');
      ['clipTextFontTool','clipTextStrokeTool','clipTextShadowTool','clipTextColorTool','clipTextBackgroundTool'].forEach(toolId=>setDisplayStable($(toolId),'none'));
      setDisplayStable(key,'flex');
      setDisplayStable(anim,'flex');
    }
  }
  const oldSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof oldSync==='function'&&!oldSync.__evv80Toolbar){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldSync.apply(this,arguments);stabilizeBottomTools();return r;};
    wrapped.__evv80Toolbar=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=wrapped;
  }
  const oldSelect=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof oldSelect==='function'&&!oldSelect.__evv80Toolbar){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldSelect.apply(this,arguments);scheduleToolbarStabilize();return r;};
    wrapped.__evv80Toolbar=true;window.selectClipForChrome=wrapped;
  }
  const oldDeselect=window.deselectAllClips;
  if(typeof oldDeselect==='function'&&!oldDeselect.__evv80Toolbar){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldDeselect.apply(this,arguments);scheduleToolbarStabilize();return r;};
    wrapped.__evv80Toolbar=true;window.deselectAllClips=wrapped;
  }
  // V80 is the sole bottom-toolbar visibility owner. Other text/audio helpers may still
  // change individual tool styles, so reconcile those changes through one coalesced RAF.
  // setDisplayStable() above is idempotent: this observer's own writes settle immediately
  // instead of fighting another writer or scheduling a frame-by-frame style ping-pong.
  let toolbarStabilizeRaf=0;
  function scheduleToolbarStabilize(){
    if(toolbarStabilizeRaf)return;
    toolbarStabilizeRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{toolbarStabilizeRaf=0;stabilizeBottomTools();});
  }
  const clipToolbar=$('bottomToolsClip');
  if(clipToolbar&&typeof MutationObserver==='function'){
    const toolbarObserver=new MutationObserver(scheduleToolbarStabilize);
    toolbarObserver.observe(clipToolbar,{attributes:true,subtree:true,attributeFilter:['style','class']});
  }

  // Pin the thin blue playhead over the complete visible timeline height.
  function restoreTimelinePlayhead(){
    const scroller=$('timelineScroll'),wrap=scroller&&scroller.querySelector(':scope > .playhead-wrap'),line=$('playhead');
    if(!scroller||!line)return;
    if(wrap){wrap.style.setProperty('visibility','visible','important');wrap.style.setProperty('opacity','1','important');}
    line.style.setProperty('display','block','important');line.style.setProperty('visibility','visible','important');
    line.style.setProperty('opacity','1','important');line.style.setProperty('width','2px','important');
    line.style.setProperty('height',Math.max(1,scroller.clientHeight)+'px','important');
    line.style.setProperty('background','#2F5CE8','important');
  }
  const oldSyncPlayhead=window.syncPlayheadHeight;
  if(typeof oldSyncPlayhead==='function'&&!oldSyncPlayhead.__evv80){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldSyncPlayhead.apply(this,arguments);restoreTimelinePlayhead();return r;};
    wrapped.__evv80=true;window.syncPlayheadHeight=wrapped;
  }
  if(typeof ResizeObserver==='function'){
    const scroller=$('timelineScroll');if(scroller){const ro=new ResizeObserver(restoreTimelinePlayhead);ro.observe(scroller);}
  }
  if(typeof MutationObserver==='function'){
    const inner=$('timelineInner');if(inner){const mo=new MutationObserver(()=>requestAnimationFrame(restoreTimelinePlayhead));mo.observe(inner,{childList:true,subtree:true});}
  }
  window.addEventListener('resize',restoreTimelinePlayhead,{passive:true});

  // During pointer-drag of a text object, keep the black lower-left resize handle on the
  // selection frame visible and re-anchor it after the editor's own per-frame box update.
  let textPointer=null,dragRaf=0,textPointerClipId=null;
  function scheduleTextHandleRepair(){
    /* v231: the active drag painter already updates its own selection box. */
    if(window.__evTextDragActive)return;
    if(dragRaf)return;
    dragRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
      dragRaf=0;if(textPointer===null)return;
      // During a timeline scrub, the playhead can briefly pass outside the selected text
      // clip's active time. The editor then hides its selection overlay for that frame;
      // hold the last valid frame's handles in place until the pointer is released.
      const id=textPointerClipId||getSelectedId(),ov=getOverlay(id);if(!ov||ov.type!=='text')return;
      const layer=$('selBoxLayer');if(!layer)return;
      layer.classList.add('evv80-text-dragging');
      layer.style.setProperty('display','block','important');
      const scale=$('sbScale'),move=$('sbTextMove');
      if(scale)scale.style.setProperty('display','flex','important');
      if(typeof window.__ethvelleRepairLayoutCorners==='function')window.__ethvelleRepairLayoutCorners();
      else if(typeof window.updateSelBox==='function')window.updateSelBox();
    });
  }
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    // Only canvas text-object gestures need the selection-handle repair. A touch that starts
    // on a timeline text clip is a scrub/clip gesture; running updateSelBox + corner repair
    // during every move there caused the multi-layer text-row lag on Android.
    const stageTextTarget=e.target&&e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#overlayMediaLayer .ethvelle-overlay-text-content,#sbTextMove,#sbScale');
    if(!stageTextTarget)return;
    const textId=getSelectedId();
    const model=getOverlay(textId);
    if(model&&model.type==='text'){
      textPointer=e.pointerId;textPointerClipId=textId;scheduleTextHandleRepair();
    }
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(textPointer===e.pointerId)scheduleTextHandleRepair();},true);
  function endTextPointer(e){if(textPointer!==e.pointerId)return;textPointer=null;textPointerClipId=null;const layer=$('selBoxLayer');if(layer)layer.classList.remove('evv80-text-dragging');requestAnimationFrame(()=>{if(typeof window.__ethvelleRepairLayoutCorners==='function')window.__ethvelleRepairLayoutCorners();});}
  document.addEventListener('pointerup',endTextPointer,true);
  document.addEventListener('pointercancel',endTextPointer,true);

  // Initial and subsequent syncs. renderFrame keeps the HUD time/play state current while
  // the same compact cluster remains visible in the normal preview and in zoom.
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>{stabilizeBottomTools();restoreTimelinePlayhead();window.ethvelleRefreshTextSwatches();if(window.ethvelleRefreshDockSwatches)window.ethvelleRefreshDockSwatches();});
})();
</script>

<!-- Ethvelle v81: zoom HUD confined to zoomed canvas only, rotation auto-fullscreen,
     and tidied selection-rail chrome (no outer shadow lines, slightly thicker center tick). -->
<style id="ethvelle-v81-final-fixes-css">
  /* The play/pause/scrub HUD belongs to the zoomed canvas only. Without this guard the HUD
     element (a sibling appended once into .canvas-wrap and then left in the DOM) stayed
     visible and interactive on top of the NORMAL canvas after exiting zoom, duplicating the
     transport-row's own play/pause/slider. Hide it whenever .canvas-wrap isn't zoomed. */
  #screen-editor .canvas-wrap:not(.evca-inline-zoom) #evcaInlineZoomHud{
    display:none!important;visibility:hidden!important;opacity:0!important;pointer-events:none!important;
  }
  /* Prevent browser scroll anchoring from shifting the editor when a first clip/row is
     selected or its live markup is rebuilt. Scroll position is managed by the editor. */
  html body #screen-editor #timelineScroll,
  html body #screen-editor #timelineInner,
  html body #screen-editor #characterRows,

  html body #screen-editor .layer-row,
  html body #screen-editor .layer-track{overflow-anchor:none!important;}

<script id="ethvelle-v81-final-fixes-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);

  // Rotating the phone to landscape while working on the canvas should behave like a video
  // player going fullscreen: automatically enter the existing inline-zoom canvas view (which
  // already fills the viewport edge-to-edge and hides the timeline/transport/bottom tools),
  // and automatically leave it again on rotating back to portrait. Previously rotation only
  // re-fit the SAME small canvas within the still-visible normal editor chrome, leaving large
  // black bars instead of filling the rotated screen.
  let autoRotateZoomArmed=false; // true only once the device is confirmed in landscape
  function editorIsActive(){
    const screen=$('screen-editor');
    return !!(screen&&screen.classList.contains('active'));
  }
  function deviceIsLandscape(){
    if(window.matchMedia){
      try{return window.matchMedia('(orientation: landscape)').matches;}catch(_e){}
    }
    return window.innerWidth>window.innerHeight;
  }
  function handleAutoRotateZoom(){
    if(!editorIsActive())return;
    // Orientation is a viewport change, not a command to enter or exit zoom.
    // Keep the user's explicit zoom state exactly as-is. The dedicated viewport-fit
    // observer below recomputes the 16:9 stage size while this class remains attached.
    // Preserve the user's explicit zoom state. The v82 viewport observer below already
    // refits the canvas on resize/orientationchange; do not trigger a second canvas rebuild.
    autoRotateZoomArmed=!!document.querySelector('#screen-editor .canvas-wrap.evca-inline-zoom');
  }
  let autoRotateRaf=0;
  function scheduleAutoRotateZoom(){
    if(autoRotateRaf)return;
    autoRotateRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{autoRotateRaf=0;handleAutoRotateZoom();});
  }
  window.addEventListener('resize',scheduleAutoRotateZoom,{passive:true});
  window.addEventListener('orientationchange',scheduleAutoRotateZoom,{passive:true});
  if(window.visualViewport)window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize',scheduleAutoRotateZoom,{passive:true});
  // Also check once on load in case the editor is opened while already in landscape.
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(scheduleAutoRotateZoom,50),{once:true});
  else setTimeout(scheduleAutoRotateZoom,50);
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v82-viewport-fit-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const screen=document.getElementById('screen-editor');
  const wrap=screen&&screen.querySelector('.canvas-wrap');
  const stage=document.getElementById('canvasStage');
  if(!screen||!wrap||!stage)return;

  let fitRaf=0;
  function activeRatio(){
    try{
      const value=(typeof currentProject!=='undefined'&&currentProject&&currentProject.ratio)||'16:9';
      const match=String(value).match(/^\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*:\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*$/);
      return match&&Number(match[1])>0&&Number(match[2])>0 ? `${Number(match[1])}:${Number(match[2])}` : '16:9';
    }catch(_e){return '16:9';}
  }
  function refit(){
    fitRaf=0;
    if(!screen.classList.contains('active'))return;
    const ratio=activeRatio();
    const parts=ratio.split(':').map(Number);
    const rw=parts[0],rh=parts[1];
    if(!(rw>0&&rh>0))return;

    // Set the non-shrinking flex behaviour before measuring/re-sizing. setupCanvasStage
    // computes the largest frame that fits both available dimensions while preserving ratio.
    stage.style.flex='0 0 auto';
    stage.style.aspectRatio=rw+' / '+rh;
    if(typeof setupCanvasStage==='function')setupCanvasStage(ratio);

    // Wait for the WebView to commit the final post-rotation layout before repainting.
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
      if(!screen.classList.contains('active'))return;
      // renderFrame already invokes drawStick, so avoid drawing the stage twice on rotation.
      try{if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();}catch(_e){}
      try{if(typeof updateSelBox==='function')updateSelBox();}catch(_e){}
    });
  }
  function scheduleRefit(){
    if(fitRaf)return;
    // Two frames let Android/WebView finish applying the new visual viewport dimensions.
    fitRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
      fitRaf=requestAnimationFrame(refit);
    });
  }

  // The observer catches the actual editor preview-area resize, including address-bar and
  // orientation changes that do not always arrive as a reliable window.resize event.
  if(typeof ResizeObserver==='function'){
    const observer=new ResizeObserver(scheduleRefit);
    observer.observe(wrap);
  }
  window.addEventListener('resize',scheduleRefit,{passive:true});
  window.addEventListener('orientationchange',scheduleRefit,{passive:true});
  if(window.visualViewport)window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize',scheduleRefit,{passive:true});

  // Re-fit after the editor becomes visible; the initial observer notification can occur
  // while its parent screen is display:none.
  const editorObserver=new MutationObserver(()=>{
    if(screen.classList.contains('active'))scheduleRefit();
  });
  editorObserver.observe(screen,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
  scheduleRefit();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v84-transform-and-timeline-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const own=(o,k)=>Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(o||{},k);
  const cloneTransform=v=>{const x=v||{};return {scale:Number(x.scale==null?1:x.scale),offsetX:Number(x.offsetX)||0,offsetY:Number(x.offsetY)||0,rotation:Number(x.rotation)||0};};
  const selectedId=()=>{try{return typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'?selectedClip:null;}catch(_e){return null;}};
  const objectClip=id=>{
    if(id==null)return null;
    if(typeof window.ethvelleObjectClipById==='function')return window.ethvelleObjectClipById(id);
    const key=String(id);
    const bgList=typeof backgrounds!=='undefined'?backgrounds:(window.backgrounds||[]);
    const ovList=typeof overlays!=='undefined'?overlays:(window.overlays||[]);
    return (Array.isArray(bgList)?bgList.find(x=>String(x.id)===key):null)
      ||(Array.isArray(ovList)?ovList.find(x=>String(x.id)===key):null);
  };
  const nowTime=()=>typeof window.getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?Number(window.getCurrentAbsoluteTime()):Number(window.currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
  const currentTransform=(clip,t)=>{
    if(!clip)return {scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0};
    const animated=typeof window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue==='function'?window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(clip,'transform',t):null;
    return cloneTransform(animated||clip.transform||{});
  };
  // Resolve an EXISTING transform key only when the playhead is on its diamond.
  // Editing elsewhere never creates a keyframe implicitly.
  function transformKeyAtPlayhead(clip,t){
    const keys=Array.isArray(clip&&clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes)?clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes:[];
    const tolerance=Math.max(0.001,typeof window.ethvelleObjectKeyframeContactWindow==='function'?Number(window.ethvelleObjectKeyframeContactWindow()):0.05);
    const chosen=window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe;
    if(chosen&&String(chosen.id)===String(clip&&clip.id)&&Number.isInteger(Number(chosen.index))){
      const candidate=keys[Number(chosen.index)];
      if(candidate&&candidate.property==='transform'&&Math.abs(Number(candidate.t)-Number(t))<=tolerance)return candidate;
    }
    return keys.reduce((best,item)=>item&&item.property==='transform'&&Number.isFinite(Number(item.t))&&Math.abs(Number(item.t)-Number(t))<=tolerance&&(!best||Math.abs(Number(item.t)-Number(t))<Math.abs(Number(best.t)-Number(t)))?item:best,null);
  }
  function editableTransform(clip,t){
    if(!clip)return null;
    if(!clip.transform)clip.transform={scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0};
    const property=typeof window.ethvelleObjectKeyframeProperty==='function'?window.ethvelleObjectKeyframeProperty(clip):'transform';
    if(property!=='transform')return clip.transform;
    const key=transformKeyAtPlayhead(clip,t);
    if(key){
      const idx=(clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes||[]).indexOf(key);
      if(idx>=0)window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe={id:String(clip.id),index:idx,source:'playhead'};
      if(!key.value||typeof key.value!=='object')key.value=cloneTransform(clip.transform);
      return key.value;
    }
    return clip.transform;
  }
  // Apply an edit without inserting a new marker. On a keyframe, only that existing key is
  // changed. Between keyframes, apply the requested transform delta to the entire authored
  // path and base value, preserving the animation curve and the original key count.
  function applyTransformEdit(clip,t,desired){
    if(!clip)return null;
    if(!clip.transform)clip.transform={scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0};
    const wanted=cloneTransform(desired),key=transformKeyAtPlayhead(clip,t);
    if(key){
      key.value=wanted;
      const idx=(clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes||[]).indexOf(key);
      if(idx>=0)window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe={id:String(clip.id),index:idx,source:'playhead'};
      return key.value;
    }
    const keys=Array.isArray(clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes)?clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes:[];
    const transformKeys=keys.filter(k=>k&&k.property==='transform'&&k.value&&typeof k.value==='object');
    if(transformKeys.length){
      const before=currentTransform(clip,t);
      const ratio=Math.max(.0001,wanted.scale)/Math.max(.0001,before.scale);
      const dx=wanted.offsetX-before.offsetX,dy=wanted.offsetY-before.offsetY;
      const dr=((wanted.rotation-before.rotation+540)%360)-180;
      const shift=value=>{
        const v=cloneTransform(value);
        v.scale=Math.max(.0001,v.scale*ratio);v.offsetX+=dx;v.offsetY+=dy;v.rotation+=dr;
        return v;
      };
      transformKeys.forEach(k=>{k.value=shift(k.value);});
      clip.transform=shift(clip.transform);
      if(clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframeBase&&clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframeBase.transform&&typeof clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframeBase.transform==='object'){
        clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframeBase.transform=shift(clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframeBase.transform);
      }
      return currentTransform(clip,t);
    }
    clip.transform=wanted;
    return clip.transform;
  }
  function refreshObjectMarkers(clip){
    if(!clip)return;
    try{if(typeof window.ethvelleRefreshObjectKeyframeTracks==='function')window.ethvelleRefreshObjectKeyframeTracks(clip.id);}catch(_e){}
    try{if(typeof window.updateKeyButtonState==='function')window.updateKeyButtonState();}catch(_e){}
  }
  function applyObjectTransform(clip,kind){
    const t=nowTime();
    if(kind==='background'){
      try{if(typeof window.refreshCurrentBgFromPlayhead==='function')window.refreshCurrentBgFromPlayhead();}catch(_e){}
      try{if(typeof window.applyBgTransform==='function')window.applyBgTransform();}catch(_e){}
    }else{
      try{if(typeof window.renderOverlayMedia==='function')window.renderOverlayMedia(t);}catch(_e){}
      try{if(typeof window.updateSelBox==='function')window.updateSelBox();}catch(_e){}
    }
  }

  // The base Transform panel used to write clip.transform even when animated transform
  // keyframes override that base. Make its values reflect and edit the live playhead value.
  const originalSync=window.syncTransformSliders;
  window.syncTransformSliders=function(){
    let target=null;try{target=typeof window.transformTarget==='function'?window.transformTarget():null;}catch(_e){}
    if(!target||!['background','overlay'].includes(target.kind)){
      if(typeof originalSync==='function')return originalSync.apply(this,arguments);
      return;
    }
    const clip=target.kind==='background'?(target.bgClip||objectClip(selectedId())):(target.ov||objectClip(selectedId()));
    if(!clip){if(typeof originalSync==='function')return originalSync.apply(this,arguments);return;}
    const v=currentTransform(clip,nowTime());
    const size=$('sizeSlider'),px=$('posXSlider'),py=$('posYSlider'),rot=$('rotSlider');
    if(!size||!px||!py||!rot)return;
    size.value=Math.max(Number(size.min)||10,Math.min(Number(size.max)||400,Math.round(v.scale*100)));
    px.value=Math.round((v.offsetX/(Math.max(1,(typeof stageW!=='undefined'?Number(stageW):1)||1)*0.75))*100)||0;
    py.value=Math.round((v.offsetY/(Math.max(1,(typeof stageH!=='undefined'?Number(stageH):1)||1)*0.75))*100)||0;
    rot.value=Math.round(v.rotation||0);
    if($('sizeVal'))$('sizeVal').textContent=size.value+'%';
    if($('posXVal'))$('posXVal').textContent=px.value;
    if($('posYVal'))$('posYVal').textContent=py.value;
    if($('rotVal'))$('rotVal').textContent=rot.value+'°';
  };

  const originalUpdate=window.updateTransform;
  window.updateTransform=function(){
    let target=null;try{target=typeof window.transformTarget==='function'?window.transformTarget():null;}catch(_e){}
    if(!target||!['background','overlay'].includes(target.kind)){
      if(typeof originalUpdate==='function')return originalUpdate.apply(this,arguments);
      return;
    }
    const clip=target.kind==='background'?(target.bgClip||objectClip(selectedId())):(target.ov||objectClip(selectedId()));
    if(!clip){if(typeof originalUpdate==='function')return originalUpdate.apply(this,arguments);return;}
    const size=Number($('sizeSlider')&&$('sizeSlider').value)||100;
    const px=Number($('posXSlider')&&$('posXSlider').value)||0;
    const py=Number($('posYSlider')&&$('posYSlider').value)||0;
    const rot=Number($('rotSlider')&&$('rotSlider').value)||0;
    const desired={scale:size/100,offsetX:(px/100)*(Math.max(1,(typeof stageW!=='undefined'?Number(stageW):1)||1)*0.75),offsetY:(py/100)*(Math.max(1,(typeof stageH!=='undefined'?Number(stageH):1)||1)*0.75),rotation:rot};
    applyTransformEdit(clip,nowTime(),desired);
    if($('sizeVal'))$('sizeVal').textContent=size+'%';
    if($('posXVal'))$('posXVal').textContent=px;
    if($('posYVal'))$('posYVal').textContent=py;
    if($('rotVal'))$('rotVal').textContent=rot+'°';
    applyObjectTransform(clip,target.kind);
    if(typeof window.debouncedPersist==='function')window.debouncedPersist();
    // Refresh the keyframe marker after the current input frame, not on every layout step.
    if(!clip.__evv84MarkerQueued){clip.__evv84MarkerQueued=true;requestAnimationFrame(()=>{clip.__evv84MarkerQueued=false;refreshObjectMarkers(clip);});}
  };

  const originalReset=window.resetTransform;
  window.resetTransform=function(){
    let target=null;try{target=typeof window.transformTarget==='function'?window.transformTarget():null;}catch(_e){}
    if(!target||!['background','overlay'].includes(target.kind)){
      if(typeof originalReset==='function')return originalReset.apply(this,arguments);
      return;
    }
    const clip=target.kind==='background'?(target.bgClip||objectClip(selectedId())):(target.ov||objectClip(selectedId()));
    if(!clip){if(typeof originalReset==='function')return originalReset.apply(this,arguments);return;}
    applyTransformEdit(clip,nowTime(),{scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0});
    if(typeof window.syncTransformSliders==='function')window.syncTransformSliders();
    applyObjectTransform(clip,target.kind);refreshObjectMarkers(clip);
    if(typeof window.persistProjectState==='function')window.persistProjectState();
    if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast('Transform reset',{icon:window.toastIcons&&window.toastIcons.check,duration:1200});
  };

  // On-canvas selection box used an interpolated clone for keyframed clips. Commit that edited
  // value back into the actual keyframe (or a new playhead key) before repainting the stage.
  const originalSelBoxApply=window.selBoxApply;
  if(typeof originalSelBoxApply==='function')window.selBoxApply=function(tg){
    if(tg&&(tg.kind==='background'||tg.kind==='overlay')){
      const clip=objectClip(selectedId());
      if(clip&&tg.obj){
        const desired=cloneTransform(tg.obj);
        const live=applyTransformEdit(clip,nowTime(),desired);
        if(live)tg.obj=live;
      }
    }
    return originalSelBoxApply.apply(this,arguments);
  };
  const originalFinishSelBox=window.finishSelBoxEdit;
  if(typeof originalFinishSelBox==='function')window.finishSelBoxEdit=function(tg){
    const result=originalFinishSelBox.apply(this,arguments);
    if(tg&&(tg.kind==='background'||tg.kind==='overlay'))refreshObjectMarkers(objectClip(selectedId()));
    return result;
  };

  // Zoom scrub must not leave the timeline's drag/pinch state armed after the HUD closes.
  const originalZoomToggle=window.toggleCanvasZoomView;
  if(typeof originalZoomToggle==='function'&&!originalZoomToggle.__evv84Recovery){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const wasZoom=!!document.querySelector('#screen-editor .canvas-wrap.evca-inline-zoom');
      const result=originalZoomToggle.apply(this,arguments);
      const isZoom=!!document.querySelector('#screen-editor .canvas-wrap.evca-inline-zoom');
      if(wasZoom&&!isZoom){
        try{if(typeof window.cancelScheduledTimelineScrub==='function')window.cancelScheduledTimelineScrub();}catch(_e){}
        window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing=false;delete window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubMaxDuration;
        try{if(typeof timelineScrollOwner!=='undefined')timelineScrollOwner='programmatic';}catch(_e){}
        try{if(typeof _timelinePinchPointers!=='undefined'&&_timelinePinchPointers.clear)_timelinePinchPointers.clear();}catch(_e){}
        try{if(typeof timelinePinchCandidate!=='undefined')timelinePinchCandidate=null;}catch(_e){}
        try{if(typeof timelineZoomGesture!=='undefined')timelineZoomGesture=null;}catch(_e){}
        try{if(typeof _timelinePinchEnding!=='undefined')_timelinePinchEnding=false;}catch(_e){}
        try{if(typeof activeClipDragCount!=='undefined')activeClipDragCount=0;}catch(_e){}
        try{if(typeof bgHandleDragActive!=='undefined')bgHandleDragActive=false;}catch(_e){}
        $('timelineInner')?.classList.remove('clip-drag-active');
        document.querySelectorAll('#timelineInner .dragging,#timelineInner .trim-gesture-active,#timelineInner [data-ev-timeline-drag-active="1"]').forEach(el=>{
          el.classList.remove('dragging','trim-gesture-active');el.removeAttribute('data-ev-timeline-drag-active');
        });
        const scroller=$('timelineScroll');
        if(scroller){scroller.style.pointerEvents='auto';scroller.style.touchAction='pan-x pan-y';scroller.style.webkitOverflowScrolling='touch';scroller.style.overflowX='auto';scroller.style.overflowY='auto';}
        requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
          if(scroller){scroller.style.pointerEvents='auto';scroller.style.touchAction='pan-x pan-y';scroller.style.overflowX='auto';scroller.style.overflowY='auto';}
          $('timelineInner')?.classList.remove('clip-drag-active');
        }));
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evv84Recovery=true;window.toggleCanvasZoomView=wrapped;
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v85-final-interactions-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);

  // Reposition the whole transport group by the difference between the actual blue playhead
  // centre and the Play/Pause button centre, so the Play/Pause control is always exact-centre.
  function alignNormalTransport(){
    const row=document.querySelector('#screen-editor > .transport-row');
    const cluster=row&&row.querySelector('.transport-cluster');
    const play=row&&row.querySelector('#playBtn');
    if(!row||!cluster||!play||!row.getClientRects().length)return;
    const line=$('playhead'),lr=line&&line.getBoundingClientRect();
    const rr=row.getBoundingClientRect(),pr=play.getBoundingClientRect();
    if(!rr.width||!pr.width)return;
    const target=lr&&lr.width>0?lr.left+lr.width/2:rr.left+rr.width/2;
    const actual=pr.left+pr.width/2;
    const shift=parseFloat(cluster.style.getPropertyValue('--ev-play-center-shift'))||0;
    const next=shift+(target-actual);
    if(Math.abs(next-shift)>0.35)cluster.style.setProperty('--ev-play-center-shift',next.toFixed(2)+'px');
  }
  let alignRaf=0;
  function scheduleTransportAlign(){if(alignRaf)return;alignRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{alignRaf=0;alignNormalTransport();});}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',scheduleTransportAlign,{once:true});
  else scheduleTransportAlign();
  window.addEventListener('resize',scheduleTransportAlign,{passive:true});
  window.addEventListener('orientationchange',scheduleTransportAlign,{passive:true});
  if(typeof ResizeObserver==='function'){
    const ro=new ResizeObserver(scheduleTransportAlign);
    const row=document.querySelector('#screen-editor > .transport-row');if(row)ro.observe(row);
    // Do not observe the entire timeline scroller: lane-height changes during clip drag
    // would otherwise schedule layout measurements for the transport on every move frame.
  }
  if(typeof MutationObserver==='function'){
    const screen=$('screen-editor');if(screen){const mo=new MutationObserver(scheduleTransportAlign);mo.observe(screen,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class','style']});}
  }

  // Ensure object keyframe detection follows the visible diamond width (including Background
  // and Text) and keep the bottom Key action available for every object-keyframe layer.
  let objectToolSyncRaf=0;
  function scheduleObjectKeyToolSync(){
    if(objectToolSyncRaf)return;
    objectToolSyncRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{objectToolSyncRaf=0;stabilizeObjectKeyTool();});
  }
  function stabilizeObjectKeyTool(){
    const id=(typeof selectedClip==='undefined'||selectedClip==null)?'':String(selectedClip);
    const edit=$('clipTextEditTool');
    if(!id){if(edit)edit.style.display='none';return;}
    let target=null;
    if(typeof window.ethvelleObjectClipById==='function')target=window.ethvelleObjectClipById(id);
    if(!target){
      const bgList=(typeof backgrounds!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(backgrounds))?backgrounds:(Array.isArray(window.backgrounds)?window.backgrounds:[]);
      const ovList=(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays))?overlays:(Array.isArray(window.overlays)?window.overlays:[]);
      target=bgList.find(item=>item&&String(item.id)===id)||ovList.find(item=>item&&String(item.id)===id)||null;
    }
    if(!target){if(edit)edit.style.display='none';return;}
    const row=$('bottomToolsClip'),key=$('clipKeyTool');
    // Use normal inline styles, not !important. syncBottomToolsMode runs first on every
    // selection and must remain able to hide/reset these controls when switching layers.
    if(row&&(row.style.getPropertyValue('display')!=='flex'||row.style.getPropertyPriority('display')!=='important'))row.style.setProperty('display','flex','important');
    if(key)key.style.display='flex';
    if(edit)edit.style.display=target.type==='text'?'flex':'none';
    ['clipTextFontTool','clipTextStrokeTool','clipTextShadowTool','clipTextColorTool','clipTextBackgroundTool'].forEach(id=>{const el=$(id);if(el)el.style.display='none';});
  }
  const oldSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof oldSync==='function'&&!oldSync.__evv85){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldSync.apply(this,arguments);scheduleObjectKeyToolSync();return r;};
    wrapped.__evv85=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=wrapped;
  }
  const oldSelect=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof oldSelect==='function'&&!oldSelect.__evv85){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldSelect.apply(this,arguments);scheduleObjectKeyToolSync();return r;};
    wrapped.__evv85=true;window.selectClipForChrome=wrapped;
  }
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(!document.hidden)scheduleTransportAlign();});
  window.addEventListener('load',()=>{scheduleTransportAlign();stabilizeObjectKeyTool();},{once:true});
})();
</script>


<style id="ethvelle-v86-performance-and-keyframe-css">
  /* Object keyframe diamonds are shown only on the currently selected clip. Hidden tracks
     remain in the data model but do not paint, hit-test, or participate in touch handling. */
  #screen-editor #timelineInner .object-keyframe-track{
    visibility:hidden!important;opacity:0!important;pointer-events:none!important;
  }
  #screen-editor #timelineInner .overlay-clip.selected > .object-keyframe-track,
  #screen-editor #timelineInner .track-clip.selected > .object-keyframe-track{
    visibility:visible!important;opacity:1!important;pointer-events:none!important;
  }
  #screen-editor #timelineInner .object-keyframe-track .kf-marker{pointer-events:auto!important;}

  /* Smooth lane change for neighbouring clips; the actively dragged clip remains locked
     to the finger without transition lag. */
  #screen-editor #timelineInner .overlay-clip{transition:top .12s ease-out;}
  #screen-editor #timelineInner .overlay-clip.dragging{transition:top .12s ease-out!important;}

  /* The contextual tool strip uses native horizontal compositing/scrolling on Android. */
  
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsClip .tool-item{touch-action:pan-x!important;}
</style>
<script id="ethvelle-v86-live-lane-layout-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(typeof window.packIntoLanes!=='function'||typeof window.overlayRowHeightForLanes!=='function')return;
  window.applyOverlayLanes=function(groupKey){
    const row=document.querySelector(`.overlay-track-row[data-group="${groupKey}"]`);
    if(!row)return;
    const track=row.querySelector('.layer-track');
    if(!track)return;
    const source=(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays))?overlays:[];
    const clips=source.filter(o=>groupKey==='audio'?o.type==='audio':(groupKey==='text'?o.type==='text':o.type!=='audio'&&o.type!=='text'));
    const lanes=window.packIntoLanes(clips);
    const laneCount=Math.max(1,lanes.length);
    const signature=lanes.map(lane=>lane.map(o=>String(o.id)).sort().join(',')).join('|');
    if(row.dataset.evV86LaneSignature===signature)return;

    row.dataset.evV86LaneSignature=signature;
    const rowH=window.overlayRowHeightForLanes(laneCount);
    const trackH=rowH;
    if(row.style.height!==(rowH+4)+'px')row.style.height=(rowH+4)+'px';
    if(row.style.minHeight!==(rowH+4)+'px')row.style.minHeight=(rowH+4)+'px';
    if(row.style.maxHeight!=='none')row.style.maxHeight='none';
    if(track.style.height!==trackH+'px')track.style.height=trackH+'px';
    if(track.style.minHeight!==trackH+'px')track.style.minHeight=trackH+'px';
    if(track.style.maxHeight!==trackH+'px')track.style.maxHeight=trackH+'px';

    const elements=track.querySelectorAll('.overlay-clip[data-overlay-id]');
    const byId=new Map();
    for(let i=0;i<elements.length;i++)byId.set(String(elements[i].dataset.overlayId),elements[i]);
    lanes.forEach((lane,laneIndex)=>{
      const top=window.overlayLaneTop(laneIndex)+'px';
      lane.forEach(clip=>{
        const el=byId.get(String(clip.id));
        if(el&&el.style.top!==top)el.style.top=top;
      });
    });
  };
})();
</script>
<script id="ethvelle-v86-keyframe-touch-guard-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  // A keyframe diamond is a separate tap target, never a clip-body drag/scrub target.
  // Stop its pointerdown in capture phase so parent clip handlers cannot arm a long-press.
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',function(e){
    const marker=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest('#timelineInner .kf-marker'):null;
    if(marker)e.stopPropagation();
  },true);
  // Refit only; rotating the device must not call toggleCanvasZoomView. The existing v82
  // ResizeObserver recomputes canvas size while preserving the user's current zoom state.
  window.__ethvelleRefitZoomViewport=window.__ethvelleRefitZoomViewport||function(){
    const screen=document.getElementById('screen-editor');
    const wrap=screen&&screen.querySelector('.canvas-wrap');
    const stage=document.getElementById('canvasStage');
    if(!screen||!wrap||!stage||!screen.classList.contains('active'))return;
    const project=window.currentProject;
    const ratio=(project&&project.ratio)||'16:9';
    if(typeof window.setupCanvasStage==='function')window.setupCanvasStage(ratio);
    if(typeof window.renderFrame==='function')requestAnimationFrame(()=>window.renderFrame());
  };
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v88-interaction-and-tools-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const getSelectedId=()=>typeof selectedClip==='undefined'||selectedClip==null?'':String(selectedClip);
  const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,Number(v)||0));
  const ovFor=id=>{try{return (typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id))||null;}catch(_e){return null;}};
  const bgFor=id=>{try{return (typeof backgrounds!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(backgrounds)?backgrounds:[]).find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id))||null;}catch(_e){return null;}};
  const isImageSource=src=>typeof src==='string'&&(/^(?:url\(|data:image\/|blob:|https?:\/\/)/i.test(src.trim()));
  const cropFractions=c=>{
    const v=c||{};let left=clamp(v.left,0,.45),right=clamp(v.right,0,.45),top=clamp(v.top,0,.45),bottom=clamp(v.bottom,0,.45);
    if(left+right>.8){const f=.8/(left+right);left*=f;right*=f;}
    if(top+bottom>.8){const f=.8/(top+bottom);top*=f;bottom*=f;}
    return {left,top,right,bottom};
  };
  window.ethvelleCropFractions=cropFractions;

  // Keep another clip from becoming selected while a held clip is being moved.
  const oldSelect=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof oldSelect==='function'&&!oldSelect.__evv87Lock){
    const wrapped=function(which){
      const active=document.querySelector('#timelineInner .anim-clip.dragging,#timelineInner .overlay-clip.dragging,#timelineInner .track-clip.dragging,#timelineInner [data-ev-timeline-drag-active="1"]');
      let lock=String(window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionLockId||'');
      if(!lock&&active){
        if(active.dataset.overlayId)lock=String(active.dataset.overlayId);
        else if(active.dataset.bgId)lock=String(active.dataset.bgId);
        else if(active.dataset.segIdx!=null)lock='seg'+active.dataset.segIdx;
        else if(active.id==='animClipEl')lock='main';
      }
      const playheadDrivenScrub=!!window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing&&!active;
      if(lock&&String(which)!==lock&&!playheadDrivenScrub)return;
      return oldSelect.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrapped.__evv87Lock=true;window.selectClipForChrome=wrapped;
  }
  // Capture the clip identity at the start of the gesture. Pointer capture can then move
  // across neighbouring clips without allowing a hover/click side-effect to change selection.
  let selectionLockPointer=null;
  function clipRefFromTarget(target){
    const ov=target&&target.closest&&target.closest('.overlay-clip[data-overlay-id]');if(ov)return String(ov.dataset.overlayId);
    const bg=target&&target.closest&&target.closest('.track-clip[data-bg-id]');if(bg)return String(bg.dataset.bgId);
    const anim=target&&target.closest&&target.closest('.anim-clip');
    if(anim){if(anim.id==='animClipEl')return 'main';if(/^seg\d+El$/.test(anim.id))return anim.id.replace(/El$/,'');if(anim.dataset.readonlyClipRef)return String(anim.dataset.readonlyClipRef);}
    return '';
  }
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    const ref=clipRefFromTarget(e.target);if(!ref)return;
    window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionLockId=ref;selectionLockPointer=e.pointerId;
  },true);
  function releaseSelectionLock(e){
    if(selectionLockPointer==null||(e&&e.pointerId!==undefined&&e.pointerId!==selectionLockPointer))return;
    selectionLockPointer=null;
    window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionLockId='';
  }
  document.addEventListener('pointerup',releaseSelectionLock,true);
  document.addEventListener('pointercancel',releaseSelectionLock,true);
  document.addEventListener('contextmenu',e=>{if(clipRefFromTarget(e.target)){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();}},true);

  // Reverse playback timestamp is shared by preview, metadata-ready and export paths.
  window.ethvelleVideoSourceTime=function(ov,t,duration){
    const speed=Math.max(.05,Number(ov&&ov.speed)||1);
    const max=Math.max(.05,(Number(duration)||Number(ov&&ov.sourceDuration)||Number(ov&&ov.trimEnd)-Number(ov&&ov.trimStart))-.02);
    const elapsed=Math.max(0,(Number(t)-Number(ov&&ov.trimStart))*speed);
    const span=Math.max(0,(Number(ov&&ov.trimEnd)-Number(ov&&ov.trimStart))*speed);
    const raw=ov&&ov.reversePlayback?(Number(ov.sourceOffset)||0)+span-elapsed:(Number(ov&&ov.sourceOffset)||0)+elapsed;
    return clamp(raw,0,max);
  };
  window.toggleSelectedVideoReverse=function(){
    const id=getSelectedId(),ov=ovFor(id);
    if(!ov||ov.type!=='video'){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Select a video clip to reverse.',{icon:window.toastIcons&&window.toastIcons.info});return;}
    ov.reversePlayback=!ov.reversePlayback;
    const el=(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls)?overlayMediaEls[id]:null;
    if(el){try{el.pause();el.muted=!!ov.reversePlayback;el.currentTime=window.ethvelleVideoSourceTime(ov,typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec!=='undefined'?Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec):0),el.duration);}catch(_e){}}
    if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();
    if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});
    if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast(ov.reversePlayback?'Reverse enabled':'Reverse disabled',{icon:window.toastIcons&&window.toastIcons.check,duration:1200});
    syncMediaTools();
  };

  // Add Reverse/Crop actions to the existing contextual layer toolbar, not as duplicate rows.
  function ensureMediaTools(){
    const row=$('bottomToolsClip');if(!row)return;
    if(!$('clipReverseTool')){
      const ref=$('clipTrimTool')||$('clipSpeedTool');
      const html=`<div class="tool-item" id="clipReverseTool" onclick="toggleSelectedVideoReverse()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M4 7v5h5M20 17v-5h-5"/><path d="M6.5 16A7 7 0 0 0 19 12M17.5 8A7 7 0 0 0 5 12"/></svg><span>Reverse</span></div>`;
      if(ref)ref.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend',html);else row.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',html);
    }
    if(!$('clipCropTool')){
      const ref=$('clipReverseTool')||$('clipTrimTool');
      const html=`<div class="tool-item" id="clipCropTool" onclick="openPanel('crop')"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M7 3v13a1 1 0 0 0 1 1h13"/><path d="M3 7h13a1 1 0 0 1 1 1v13"/><path d="M7 7h10v10"/></svg><span>Crop</span></div>`;
      if(ref)ref.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend',html);else row.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',html);
    }
  }
  function syncMediaTools(){
    ensureMediaTools();
    const id=getSelectedId(),ov=ovFor(id),bg=bgFor(id);
    const reverse=$('clipReverseTool'),crop=$('clipCropTool');
    if(reverse)reverse.style.display=ov&&ov.type==='video'?'flex':'none';
    if(crop)crop.style.display=(ov&&(ov.type==='video'||ov.type==='image'))||(bg&&isImageSource(bg.src))?'flex':'none';
  }
  const oldSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof oldSync==='function'&&!oldSync.__evv87Media){window.syncBottomToolsMode=function(){const r=oldSync.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(syncMediaTools);return r;};window.syncBottomToolsMode.__evv87Media=true;}
  const oldSelect2=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof oldSelect2==='function'&&!oldSelect2.__evv87Media){window.selectClipForChrome=function(){const r=oldSelect2.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(syncMediaTools);return r;};window.selectClipForChrome.__evv87Media=true;}
  const oldDeselect=window.deselectAllClips;
  if(typeof oldDeselect==='function'&&!oldDeselect.__evv87Media){window.deselectAllClips=function(){const r=oldDeselect.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(syncMediaTools);return r;};window.deselectAllClips.__evv87Media=true;}

  // Tick-ruler Transform panel. Existing slider IDs and updateTransform() API are preserved.
  function rulerRow(label,id,valueId,min,max,step,suffix){
    return `<div class="evtr-slider-row"><div class="evtr-slider-head"><span>${label}</span><span class="evtr-slider-value" id="${valueId}">0${suffix||''}</span></div><div class="evtr-ruler" data-evtr-ruler="${id}"><input type="range" id="${id}" min="${min}" max="${max}" step="${step||1}" value="0" oninput="updateTransform();ethvelleSyncTransformRulers()" aria-label="${label}"></div></div>`;
  }
  function transformBody(){return `<div class="evv-transform-slider-panel">
    <div class="transform-group">
      <div class="transform-label-row"><span>Size</span><span class="transform-value" id="sizeVal">100%</span></div>
      <input type="range" class="transform-slider" id="sizeSlider" min="10" max="400" step="1" value="100" oninput="updateTransform()" aria-label="Size">
    </div>
    <div class="transform-group">
      <div class="transform-label-row"><span>Horizontal position</span><span class="transform-value" id="posXVal">0</span></div>
      <input type="range" class="transform-slider" id="posXSlider" min="-200" max="200" step="1" value="0" oninput="updateTransform()" aria-label="Horizontal position">
    </div>
    <div class="transform-group">
      <div class="transform-label-row"><span>Vertical position</span><span class="transform-value" id="posYVal">0</span></div>
      <input type="range" class="transform-slider" id="posYSlider" min="-200" max="200" step="1" value="0" oninput="updateTransform()" aria-label="Vertical position">
    </div>
    <div class="transform-group">
      <div class="transform-label-row"><span>Rotation</span><span class="transform-value" id="rotVal">0°</span></div>
      <input type="range" class="transform-slider" id="rotSlider" min="-180" max="180" step="1" value="0" oninput="updateTransform()" aria-label="Rotation">
    </div>
    <button type="button" class="transform-reset-btn" onclick="resetTransform()">Reset transform</button>
  </div>`;}
  function syncTransformRulers(){
    ['posXSlider','posYSlider','sizeSlider','rotSlider'].forEach(id=>{
      const input=$(id),wrap=document.querySelector(`[data-evtr-ruler="${id}"]`);if(!input||!wrap)return;
      const min=Number(input.min)||0,max=Number(input.max)||100,value=Number(input.value)||0;
      const pct=max>min?Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(value-min)/(max-min))):.5;
      const usable=Math.max(1,(wrap.clientWidth||1)-20);
      wrap.style.setProperty('--evtr-progress',pct.toFixed(4));
      wrap.style.setProperty('--evtr-shift',((.5-pct)*usable).toFixed(2)+'px');
    });
    const sv=$('sizeVal'),xv=$('posXVal'),yv=$('posYVal'),rv=$('rotVal');
    if(sv&&$('sizeSlider'))sv.textContent=$('sizeSlider').value+'%';
    if(xv&&$('posXSlider'))xv.textContent=$('posXSlider').value;
    if(yv&&$('posYSlider'))yv.textContent=$('posYSlider').value;
    if(rv&&$('rotSlider'))rv.textContent=$('rotSlider').value+'°';
  }
  window.ethvelleSyncTransformRulers=syncTransformRulers;
  window.ethvelleTransformTab=function(name){
    document.querySelectorAll('#toolPanel .evtr-tab').forEach(el=>el.classList.toggle('active',el.dataset.evtrTab===name));
    document.querySelectorAll('#toolPanel .evtr-pane').forEach(el=>el.classList.toggle('active',el.dataset.evtrPane===name));
  };
  if(typeof panelContent!=='undefined'&&panelContent.transform)panelContent.transform.body=transformBody();
  const oldOpen=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof oldOpen==='function'&&!oldOpen.__evv87Transform){
    window.openPanel=function(key){
      const r=oldOpen.apply(this,arguments);
      if(key==='transform'){window.ethvelleTransformTab('position');requestAnimationFrame(()=>{if(typeof window.syncTransformSliders==='function')window.syncTransformSliders();syncTransformRulers();});}
      if(key==='crop')requestAnimationFrame(syncCropPanel);
      return r;
    };
    window.openPanel.__evv87Transform=true;
  }
  const oldSyncTransform=window.syncTransformSliders;
  if(typeof oldSyncTransform==='function'&&!oldSyncTransform.__evv87Ruler){
    window.syncTransformSliders=function(){const r=oldSyncTransform.apply(this,arguments);syncTransformRulers();return r;};
    window.syncTransformSliders.__evv87Ruler=true;
  }
  const oldUpdateTransform=window.updateTransform;
  if(typeof oldUpdateTransform==='function'&&!oldUpdateTransform.__evv87Ruler){
    window.updateTransform=function(){const r=oldUpdateTransform.apply(this,arguments);syncTransformRulers();return r;};
    window.updateTransform.__evv87Ruler=true;
  }

  // Crop editor data model: normalized fractions are applied in live preview and in export.
  function currentCropTarget(){const id=getSelectedId();const ov=ovFor(id),bg=bgFor(id);if(ov&&(ov.type==='video'||ov.type==='image'))return {kind:'overlay',clip:ov};if(bg&&isImageSource(bg.src))return {kind:'background',clip:bg};return null;}
  function cropBody(){return `<div class="evcr-root">
    ${[['Left','left'],['Top','top'],['Right','right'],['Bottom','bottom']].map(([label,key])=>`<div class="evcr-row"><div class="evcr-head"><span>${label}</span><span id="evcr-${key}-value">0%</span></div><input type="range" id="evcr-${key}" min="0" max="45" step="1" value="0" oninput="ethvelleApplyCropFromControls()"></div>`).join('')}
    <div class="evcr-footer"><button type="button" class="evtr-reset" onclick="ethvelleResetCrop()">↶ Reset crop</button><button type="button" class="evtr-done" onclick="closePanel()" aria-label="Done">Apply</button></div></div>`;}
  if(typeof panelContent!=='undefined')panelContent.crop={title:'Crop',body:cropBody()};
  function getCropTarget(){return currentCropTarget();}
  function syncCropPanel(){
    const target=getCropTarget();if(!target)return;
    const c=cropFractions(target.clip.crop);
    ['left','top','right','bottom'].forEach(k=>{const input=$('evcr-'+k),value=$('evcr-'+k+'-value');if(input)input.value=Math.round(c[k]*100);if(value)value.textContent=Math.round(c[k]*100)+'%';});
  }
  window.ethvelleApplyCropFromControls=function(){
    const target=getCropTarget();if(!target)return;
    const c=cropFractions({left:Number($('evcr-left')&&$('evcr-left').value)/100,top:Number($('evcr-top')&&$('evcr-top').value)/100,right:Number($('evcr-right')&&$('evcr-right').value)/100,bottom:Number($('evcr-bottom')&&$('evcr-bottom').value)/100});
    target.clip.crop=c;
    syncCropPanel();
    if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();
    else if(target.kind==='background'&&typeof applyBackground==='function')applyBackground();
    if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});
  };
  window.ethvelleResetCrop=function(){const target=getCropTarget();if(!target)return;target.clip.crop={left:0,top:0,right:0,bottom:0};syncCropPanel();if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});};
  function cssCropTransform(clip,transform,sourceEl,motion){
    const stage=$('canvasStage'),sw=Math.max(1,stage?stage.clientWidth:1),sh=Math.max(1,stage?stage.clientHeight:1);
    const iw=Math.max(1,sourceEl&&(sourceEl.videoWidth||sourceEl.naturalWidth)||sw),ih=Math.max(1,sourceEl&&(sourceEl.videoHeight||sourceEl.naturalHeight)||sh);
    const c=cropFractions(clip.crop),cropW=iw*(1-c.left-c.right),cropH=ih*(1-c.top-c.bottom);
    const baseContain=Math.min(sw/iw,sh/ih),hasCrop=(c.left+c.right+c.top+c.bottom)>0.0001;
    const desiredScale=hasCrop?Math.max(sw/cropW,sh/cropH):baseContain;
    const scaleFactor=desiredScale/Math.max(.0001,baseContain);
    const cropDx=(c.right-c.left)*iw*desiredScale/2,cropDy=(c.bottom-c.top)*ih*desiredScale/2;
    const tr=transform||clip.transform||{},m=motion||{};
    const ox=(Number(tr.offsetX)||0)+(Number(m.x)||0)+cropDx,oy=(Number(tr.offsetY)||0)+(Number(m.y)||0)+cropDy;
    const rotation=(Number(tr.rotation)||0)+(Number(m.rotation)||0),scale=(Number(tr.scale)||1)*(Number(m.scale)||1)*scaleFactor;
    return `translate(${ox}px,${oy}px) rotate(${rotation}deg) scale(${scale}) scaleX(${clip.mirrorX?-1:1}) scaleY(${clip.mirrorY?-1:1})`;
  }
  window.ethvelleApplyCropPreview=function(){
    const now=typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec!=='undefined'?Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec):0);
    const id=getSelectedId();
    (typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).forEach(ov=>{
      if(ov.type!=='video'&&ov.type!=='image')return;
      const start=Number(ov.trimStart)||0,end=Number(ov.trimEnd)||start;
      if(now<start-.001||now>=end+.001)return;
      const c=cropFractions(ov.crop);
      if(c.left+c.right+c.top+c.bottom<=.0001)return;
      const el=(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls)?overlayMediaEls[ov.id]:null;if(!el)return;
      const tracked=typeof window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform==='function'?window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform(ov,now):ov.transform;
      const keyed=typeof window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue==='function'?window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(ov,'transform',now):null;
      const tr=ov.clipTracking&&ov.clipTracking.enabled&&ov.clipTracking.targetId?(tracked||ov.transform):(keyed||tracked||ov.transform);
      const motion=typeof window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState==='function'?window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState(ov,now):{x:0,y:0,rotation:0,scale:1};
      const value=cssCropTransform(ov,tr,el,motion)+(window.ethvellePerspectiveSuffix?window.ethvellePerspectiveSuffix(ov):'')
      if(el.style.transform!==value)el.style.transform=value;
      const chroma=(typeof overlayChromaCanvases!=='undefined'&&overlayChromaCanvases)?overlayChromaCanvases[ov.id]:null;
      if(chroma&&chroma.style.transform!==value)chroma.style.transform=value;
    });
    const bg=typeof currentBackgroundClip==='function'?currentBackgroundClip(now):bgFor(id);
    const img=$('bgImgLayer');
    if(bg&&img&&img.style.display!=='none'){
      const c=cropFractions(bg.crop);
      if(c.left+c.right+c.top+c.bottom>.0001){
        const tr=typeof window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue==='function'?(window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(bg,'transform',now)||bg.transform):bg.transform;
        const value=cssCropTransform(bg,tr,img,null);if(img.style.transform!==value)img.style.transform=value;
      }
    }
  };
  const oldRenderOverlay=window.renderOverlayMedia;
  if(typeof oldRenderOverlay==='function'&&!oldRenderOverlay.__evv87Crop){
    window.renderOverlayMedia=function(){const r=oldRenderOverlay.apply(this,arguments);window.ethvelleApplyCropPreview();return r;};
    window.renderOverlayMedia.__evv87Crop=true;
  }
  const oldApplyBg=window.applyBackground;
  if(typeof oldApplyBg==='function'&&!oldApplyBg.__evv87Crop){
    window.applyBackground=function(){const r=oldApplyBg.apply(this,arguments);window.ethvelleApplyCropPreview();return r;};
    window.applyBackground.__evv87Crop=true;
  }
  const oldApplyBgT=window.applyBgTransform;
  if(typeof oldApplyBgT==='function'&&!oldApplyBgT.__evv87Crop){
    window.applyBgTransform=function(){const r=oldApplyBgT.apply(this,arguments);window.ethvelleApplyCropPreview();return r;};
    window.applyBgTransform.__evv87Crop=true;
  }

  // Reconcile crop tool visibility and the active selection without scanning all timeline rows.
  const oldSelect3=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof oldSelect3==='function'&&!oldSelect3.__evv87Crop){window.selectClipForChrome=function(){const r=oldSelect3.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(syncMediaTools);return r;};window.selectClipForChrome.__evv87Crop=true;}
  ensureMediaTools();syncMediaTools();
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{ensureMediaTools();syncMediaTools();},{once:true});
  else requestAnimationFrame(()=>{ensureMediaTools();syncMediaTools();});
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v89-deep-fixes-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);

  // Prevent a duplicate crop-preview pass when applyBackground and renderOverlayMedia are
  // called back-to-back in the same frame. The actual video/image decoder work remains
  // independently throttled by the existing compositor.
  let cropPreviewLastAt=0,cropPreviewLastTime=NaN;
  const oldCropPreview=window.ethvelleApplyCropPreview;
  window.ethvelleApplyCropPreview=function(){
    const now=typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?Number(getCurrentAbsoluteTime()):Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
    const stamp=performance.now();
    if(Number.isFinite(cropPreviewLastTime)&&Math.abs(now-cropPreviewLastTime)<0.00001&&stamp-cropPreviewLastAt<5)return;
    cropPreviewLastAt=stamp;cropPreviewLastTime=now;
    const editingId=window.__ethvelleCropEditingId==null?'':String(window.__ethvelleCropEditingId);
    const map=typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls?overlayMediaEls:{};
    const list=typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[];
    for(let i=0;i<list.length;i++){
      const ov=list[i];
      if(!ov||(ov.type!=='video'&&ov.type!=='image'))continue;
      const id=String(ov.id),el=map[id];if(!el||id===editingId)continue;
      const c=window.ethvelleCropFractions?window.ethvelleCropFractions(ov.crop):{left:0,right:0,top:0,bottom:0};
      if(c.left+c.right+c.top+c.bottom<=0.0001)continue;
      const tracked=typeof window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform==='function'?window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform(ov,now):ov.transform;
      const keyed=typeof window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue==='function'?window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(ov,'transform',now):null;
      const tr=ov.clipTracking&&ov.clipTracking.enabled&&ov.clipTracking.targetId?(tracked||ov.transform):(keyed||tracked||ov.transform);
      const iw=Math.max(1,el.videoWidth||el.naturalWidth||1),ih=Math.max(1,el.videoHeight||el.naturalHeight||1);
      const sw=Math.max(1,$('canvasStage')?.clientWidth||1),sh=Math.max(1,$('canvasStage')?.clientHeight||1);
      const cropW=iw*(1-c.left-c.right),cropH=ih*(1-c.top-c.bottom),base=Math.min(sw/iw,sh/ih);
      const desired=Math.max(sw/cropW,sh/cropH),factor=desired/Math.max(.0001,base);
      const dx=(c.right-c.left)*iw*desired/2,dy=(c.bottom-c.top)*ih*desired/2;
      const motion=typeof window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState==='function'?window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState(ov,now):{x:0,y:0,rotation:0,scale:1};
      const value=`translate(${(Number(tr?.offsetX)||0)+(Number(motion?.x)||0)+dx}px,${(Number(tr?.offsetY)||0)+(Number(motion?.y)||0)+dy}px) rotate(${(Number(tr?.rotation)||0)+(Number(motion?.rotation)||0)}deg) scale(${(Number(tr?.scale)||1)*(Number(motion?.scale)||1)*factor}) scaleX(${ov.mirrorX?-1:1}) scaleY(${ov.mirrorY?-1:1})`+(window.ethvellePerspectiveSuffix?window.ethvellePerspectiveSuffix(ov):'');
      if(el.style.transform!==value)el.style.transform=value;
      const chroma=typeof overlayChromaCanvases!=='undefined'&&overlayChromaCanvases?overlayChromaCanvases[id]:null;
      if(chroma&&chroma.style.transform!==value)chroma.style.transform=value;
    }
    const bg=typeof currentBackgroundClip==='function'?currentBackgroundClip(now):null;
